Chapter 3645
Luo Zheng's words caused the hearts of Tu Ye and the Scarlet Demons to sink.
The Demon Eagles were ecstatic.
In a short time, the Scarlet Demons and the Demon Eagles swapped positions.
Qiang Yu was very excited and replied with a trembling voice, "We are absolutely irreconcilable with the Scarlet Demons. Of course it's good!"
All of the Demon Eagles flapped their wings in the air, creating vibrating sounds. They would rush down from the sky at any moment!
The Scarlet Demons were facing a pincer attack!
The worst thing was that the humans still had the Holy Stone in their hands …
It was like going from heaven to hell.
Tu Ye's expression was as ugly as it could be.
Seeing that the humans and the Demon Eagles were about to attack, Tu Ye suddenly waved his hand and an exit appeared on the side of the birdcage. "Let's go!"
This birdcage treasure was bestowed to Tu Ye by the flame life form. It could be controlled at will.
The Scarlet Demons were already afraid. Now that even their chief was leading them to escape, how could they not leave?
Under Tu Ye's lead, the Scarlet Demons rushed out of the birdcage.
This time, Tu Ye led over a hundred Scarlet Demons. More than ten of them were killed when they attacked Luo Zheng. There were still eighty of them left.
Qiang Yu's eyes narrowed. "You want to leave? Not so easy! "
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
All of the Demon Eagles turned into blades again and charged at the Scarlet Demons below.
When the Scarlet Demons saw the Demon Eagles coming down, they all summoned the Black Tortoise to protect themselves.
The Black Tortoise's defense was extremely strong. When the Demon Eagles' wings slashed on it, it was difficult for them to break through the Scarlet Demons' defense in a short time.
At this time, Fuxi, Nuwa, Rat King Sheng, and the others attacked from the other side.
They didn't need to attack directly. They just used the Light of Destruction to remove the Black Tortoise on the Scarlet Demons.
The Light of Destruction shot over and caused the Black Tortoise to quickly dissipate.
Without the protection of the Black Tortoise, the Demon Eagles once again landed. Their sharp wings were like blades as they slashed and slashed at the Scarlet Demons …
In almost a breath's time, the Demon Eagles had already killed more than twenty Scarlet Demons!
The other Scarlet Demons didn't dare to continue fighting and swarmed towards the exit of the birdcage.
But before the remaining Scarlet Demons could get out, the exit of the birdcage suddenly closed …
"Patriarch?"
"Why did you close it?"
"Let us out! Don't abandon us! "
Only half of the Crimson Demons led by Tuye had escaped. There were still nearly twenty Crimson Demons in the birdcage.
The Crimson Demons trapped in the birdcage begged with all their might. The Crimson Demons outside the birdcage also looked extremely conflicted.
After all, they were of the same race, and some were even blood brothers.
Tuye was also very conflicted.
However, if he did not close the birdcage, Qiang Yu and those humans would take the opportunity to come out.
It said fiercely, "Let's leave this place first. We will definitely get our revenge!"
With that said, Tuye led the remaining Crimson Demons and fled.
The Crimson Demons in the birdcage fell into despair. Their will to fight instantly crumbled, and they were slaughtered without any decent resistance.
"Crack!"
Qiang Yu's blade-like wings slashed down from top to bottom, splitting the last Scarlet Demon into two halves. She cleaned the bloodstains on her wings and turned to look at Luo Zheng. "Is there a way to break this birdcage?"
It was not satisfied with the current results.
If it pursued them now, it could wipe out Tuye and the Crimson Demons in one fell swoop.
This time, Tuye led the elites of the race. If they were all killed, the entire Crimson Demon race would be removed from Night Vision Island.
Luo Zheng answered Qiang Yu directly. He reached out to touch the birdcage.
This birdcage contained rich space power. It was obviously not an ordinary Nirvana token.
If the origin bloodline in his body was willing to help, it should not be a problem to break the birdcage. However, the origin bloodline had been getting less and less effective recently. It did not even react unless its life was in danger. He could not count on it.
Luo Zheng turned to look at Nuwa and the others. However, he saw that Nuwa and Blood Wolf seemed to be secretly communicating something.
"Goddess Nuwa, can this birdcage be destroyed?" Luo Zheng asked.
"It should be possible." Nuwa nodded.
Qiang Yu heard Nuwa's answer and said impatiently, "Then break it now!"
"It needs time," Nuwa said again.
Qiang Yu's brows furrowed slightly when she heard that.
The Crimson Demons were not slow. If it broke the birdcage now, it was still confident that it could catch up. If it delayed for a while, how could it catch up?
In fact, the Immortal Hunters were very confident in breaking the birdcage.
The birdcage was woven with space power. As long as the Blood Crow and Blood Lion brothers worked together to summon the black sun and suck a part of the birdcage in, the entire birdcage would collapse.
Blood Wolf originally wanted to destroy the birdcage directly, but Nuwa stopped it.
The Demon Hawk race was very strong on Night Vision Island. Today, they were defeated by the Crimson Demons because of the Light of Destruction.
The Demon Hawk race did not have the Holy Stone and could not control the Light of Destruction. The next time they encountered the Crimson Demons, they would be defeated.
This way, the Demon Hawk race had no choice but to cooperate with Luo Zheng and the others!
In fact, Qiang Yu was in a hurry to catch up. It was not only to kill Tuye, but also for the Holy Stone in their hands.
Although almost half of the Crimson Demons were dead, the dead Crimson Demons did not have the Holy Stone. This was the most important thing.
"Can it be faster?" Qiang Yu asked unwillingly.
Nuwa was just about to shake her head when white fur floated down from not far away.
The appearance of the white-furred monsters could delay the time, so she pointed at the white-furred monsters.
Qiang Yu sighed when she saw the white-furred monsters.
With the arrival of the white-furred monsters, they definitely could not catch up.
Even if they were smeared with Ghost Medicine Powder, walking at night would attract white-furred monsters.
Not to mention that the battle between the three parties had shaken off a lot of Ghost Medicine Powder, which would naturally attract a lot of white-furred monsters.
When the white-furred monsters appeared, all activities had to stop. After the Demon Hawks and Luo Zheng's group quickly replenished the Ghost Medicine Powder, they stayed in place and did not move.
These white-furred monsters wandered around the Demon Hawks and Luo Zheng's group for a while. With the protection of the Ghost Medicine Powder, they could not discover these creatures. In the end, the white-furred monsters walked towards the corpses of the Crimson Demons.
The Crimson Demons had just been killed, and their blood had not yet cooled. Even the corpses were emitting rich vitality.
After the white-furred monsters swallowed the corpses, they left behind some "mystery" and then disappeared …
After such a delay, Tuye and the others had already gone far away. Qiang Yu was not in a hurry.
Some things had to be done well.
Nuwa and the others did not quickly destroy the birdcage.
Xue Lang's hands turned into thin threads, wrapping around the birdcage as he pretended.
Fuxi also condensed Eight Trigrams of various sizes on the birdcage.
After pretending to fiddle with it for a while, the brothers Xue Ya and Xue Lion took out the Black Sun.
The Black Sun's powerful gravitational force sucked in a part of the birdcage. The originally square birdcage looked a bit distorted, but it still maintained its integrity.
Then, Xue Ya and Xue Lion separated their hands, and the Black Sun disappeared.
"Hua!"
The spatial power balance inside the birdcage was broken. With a muffled sound, the spatial birdcage, which could change size, directly collapsed.
When the Demon Hawks saw the collapsing birdcage, their eyes revealed surprise.
These extremely arrogant Demon Hawks had never had any allies on Night Vision Island. They had always been very powerful and never needed the help of any other race, but now they urgently needed the help of these humans.

Chapter 3646
West of Shangqing Sky.
The sky was red for several days in a row.
A huge cloud of fire floated in the sky day and night. It could be seen clearly from thousands of miles away.
Below this ball of fire cloud was the terrifying Amber Cave, the main camp of the Wu Kong Clan.
As the most evil place in Shangqing Sky, no one was willing to go near Amber Cave.
The Yuanling Civilization was even more unwilling.
The Yuanling Race was made of energy. Once they were sucked into the white mist of Amber Cave, their energy would be directly extracted by the white mist. The Yuanling Race would wither and die from energy loss.
For the Yuanling Race, this was a tragic way to die.
There was once an Yuanling Race member who chased after the Wu Kong Clan. Before it even got close to Amber Cave, the white mist inside turned into a huge hand and pinched the Yuanling Race member. With this pinch, the Yuanling Race member disappeared into the white mist!
From then on, the Yuanling Race members never went near Amber Cave.
But today, the Yuanling Civilization launched a huge attack on Amber Cave.
On the ground on one side of Amber Cave, a huge rune was drawn. The Holy Winged Armor sat in the center of the rune.
Flames were constantly erupting from the inside of the Holy Winged Armor.
These flames followed the patterns of the rune and gathered at the very end, forming a huge fire tornado that gathered in the sky.
The huge cloud of fire in the sky came from this.
"Da da da …"
The cloud of fire in the sky trembled.
A large rain of fire fell from the cloud of fire, falling directly into the white mist.
The white mist could swallow all energy …
This rain of fire had terrifying destructive power, but it disappeared without a trace when it came into contact with the white mist.
Mu Ling stood not far away, staring at this spectacular scene. Her eyes blinked occasionally.
On the other hand, Ah Huo sat cross-legged at the side, revealing a bored expression.
She yawned. "It's been so long, but the mist in Amber Cave hasn't disappeared at all …"
"Xuchen has a huge appetite. It's not easy to feed it," Mu Ling said calmly.
Such a huge cloud of fire could be said to have gathered the power of all the fire spirits in the Yuanling Civilization.
After burning for several days, the white mist didn't have much of a reaction.
This white mist was actually the Xuchen distributed in Jade Clear Sky.
In the Jade Clear Sky, they were colorless and tasteless. As long as they were too far away from the Clear Sky Platform, they would be silently swallowed up by the Void Dust.
When the Void Dust was moved to the Amber Cave, it gained color and its form could be observed.
Just as Mu Ling finished speaking, the white mist suddenly surged.
Suddenly, dozens of fog dragons shot out from the white fog, heading straight for the fire clouds in the sky!
"Hua hua hua …"
"You finally can't hold it in anymore?" Ah Huo's eyes revealed a hint of excitement.
These mist dragons rushed straight into the sky, drilling into the fire clouds in an attempt to destroy them.
"It's not that they can't control themselves," Mu Ling said.
"Don't you want to extinguish the fire clouds?" Ah Huo asked.
Mu Ling smiled faintly, "Haha, the source of the Fire Clouds is here, but they are targeting the Fire Clouds. Why?"
"You want to take me by surprise?"
"Yes!"
Just as the two of them finished their conversation, a huge misty hand suddenly stretched out from the amber cave. This misty hand slammed down towards Mu Ling and the others at an extremely fast speed.
With the power of Void Dust, nothing would be left behind in the path of this slap.
Only a palm print would be left on the ground.
This kind of thing had happened before. Even now, the palm print still existed. Now, the palm print was filled with water, forming a palm print lake. It was also called the Five Finger Lake by the creatures of the Shangqing Sky.
Mu Ling had already anticipated the counterattack from the amber cave.
Her eyes blinked slightly, and the space was displaced.
From where she and Ah Huo were standing to where the flame runes were, the space moved back five thousand feet in an orderly manner.
"Hiss hiss …"
The gigantic hand of fog slapped down without any earth-shattering might or sound.
It sank into the ground silently, silently swallowing everything in its path.
After leaving a huge palm print on the ground, the white mist quickly flowed back into the amber cave.
When the white mist returned, Mu Ling's eyes blinked again, and the space returned to its original position.
Because the foundation below had been swallowed up by the Void Dust, Mu Ling, Ah Huo, and the Holy Winged Armor looked like they were standing on a square stone.
"Hualala …"
More than half of the fire clouds in the sky were swallowed up by the mist dragons, but with a flash of light on the surface of the Holy Winged Armor, flames flowed again in the huge runes. The fire tornado also swept up quickly, continuously sending flames into the fire clouds.
The only purpose of the Elemental Spirit civilization now was to consume.
The Void Dust could indeed swallow all matter, but it couldn't swallow indefinitely.
Even if the Void Dust had a terrible appetite, there would always be a moment when it was full.
Mu Ling had actually been waiting for the Wukong Clan's counterattack.
If the Wukong Clan didn't move, they wouldn't be afraid of the continuous fire rain at all. They didn't need to react at all. They just needed to stay in the amber cave obediently.
Anyway, the Elemental Spirit civilization couldn't break in.
But the Wukong Clan counterattacked, which in turn confirmed Mu Ling's guess.
If the fire rain kept burning, the Void Dust would be fed sooner or later.
"Let the Thunder Clan join in as well!" Mu Ling ordered.
"Yes, Lord Mu Ling," responded Claire in the Holy Garment Armor.
Immediately afterward, thunderbolts began to mix into the runes, and the edge of the fire clouds above kept emitting crackling sounds. The blue lightning plated the fire clouds, making them look more powerful.
Just as the Elemental Spirit civilization increased their offensive, a flame drifted over in the distance.
It came from afar and approached quickly.
Fire glanced at it faintly. "It's God Scorch's avatar."
After the three stone statues in the Earth Kiln were destroyed, Mu Ling sent three fire spirits to deal with them.
"Hualala …"
God Scorch's avatar came to Mu Ling and reported. "The Night Vision Island has been repaired."
All of Mu Ling's eyeballs glowed.
Fire raised his eyebrows and showed a charming smile. "Lord Mu Ling's hunch is very accurate."
Mu Ling didn't respond. She was awakening an eye on her body.
A layer of white film was attached to the surface of the eyeball. After awakening, the white film began to peel off quickly, turning into a small white snake.
The small white snake glanced at the Amber Cave and scolded Mu Ling. "Are you a good-for-nothing? It's been so long, but you haven't even taken down the Amber Cave. Shifang Prefecture has been delayed for several days! "
Fire's face was a little gloomy and fearful.
Mu Ling was a supreme existence in the eyes of all the Elemental Spirit people. Being reprimanded by this little white snake, Fire, who was loyal to Mu Ling, felt very uncomfortable.
But Fire also knew that everything about the Elemental Spirit civilization was given by them.
Whether it was the Holy Wing Armor, the treacherous fusion, or even the rise of the Elemental Spirit civilization …
When Mu Ling told Fire that these guys hiding in the depths of Jade Clear Sky were the foundation of the Elemental Spirit civilization, Fire was so shocked that she was almost speechless.

Chapter 3649
Main Clear Sky Platform …
Fog City.
The white fog was the most terrifying monster.
A very small number of strange people used unique methods to take away a very small portion of the white fog …
More white fog turned into this city, turning into streets, towers, walls, and ground.
It looked very ordinary from the outside, but the streets, towers, walls, and even the ground could turn into monsters that would devour people at any time!
On a wide street.
A snow-white snake shadow twisted its body crazily and ran at an incredible speed.
On the other side of the street, three birds spread their wings and flew.
"Wide, wide, wide …"
The snake was singing.
The song was like a sorrowful cry, summoning its companions.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
The three birds flapped their wings gently and arrived at the white snake's back at a precise location.
Their eyes were pupils, then they raised their heads and plunged into the white snake's body. The three birds actually drilled into the white snake's body.
Then, the three birds released more and more intense energy, and their bodies turned red.
"Boom, boom, boom!"
The violent explosion formed a terrifying shock wave that spread in all directions, and a huge mushroom cloud bloomed in the center of Fog City.
Everything in this city was formed by white fog.
When the terrifying shock wave spread, the walls of the nearby houses instantly distorted and turned into white fog …
However, this white fog form only lasted for a blink of an eye, and then it instantly returned to its original shape.
The huge white snake's body was blown apart by the birds. As a Shift, it could transform into various shapes, but the three birds also released a considerable amount of Light of Destruction when they self-destructed.
The Light of Destruction could deal a devastating blow to a Vengeful Shift.
How could it produce a very special change in the face of a Resentful Shift or a Shift above it?
A Resentful Shift would lose the ability to transform at will, and their bodies would become very fragile, so they could be killed.
The white snake, with only half of its body, could not return to its original shape. It became very weak and leaned against a corner of the wall.
In Fog City, only by constantly walking could one avoid being swallowed.
The white snake could no longer walk. It watched as the wall behind it softened and turned into rolling white fog that completely swallowed it.
However, it was not too afraid …
A Shift could not die in the secondary world.
Its coordinates had been fixed, and it would soon be resurrected somewhere in Jade Clear Sky.
The Phantoms of Jade Clear Sky could no longer remember how long this war of constant resurrection and death had lasted.
Almost every awakened Shift had gone through thousands of cycles of failure and failure, from death to resurrection.
In the beginning, they were very fanatical.
But when the same thing happened over and over again, no matter who it was, they would feel sick of it.
Just like this Fog City …
They had repeatedly seized it hundreds of times.
In order to consolidate their strength, they had changed the coordinates to this place immediately after seizing Fog City. However, they were still slaughtered in the end, and even the coordinates had been erased.
Seize, be seized, seize, and be seized again …
This behavior became extremely laughable and boring as it was repeated.
Wasn't it just a city that contained Void Dust?
So what if he obtained a portion of the Void Dust that could be controlled?
Would they be able to decide the outcome of this war?
Unfortunately, although these awakened ones had their own intelligence, they were still members of the Four Spirit Gates. They could only listen to the orders of the higher-ups.
Complaining won't solve anything.
Similar scenes were playing out in other parts of Fog City.
Originally, these white snakes could only hide in the north side of the Fog City. Once they crossed the central axis of the Fog City, they would be cleaned up by the birds.
Now that more and more snakes were entering the Fog City, the snakes had the upper hand!
On a tower in the south side of the Fog City, several black eagles stood tall.
The tower was also formed by the white fog.
Any living creature that approached the tower would be swallowed by the white fog, and the black eagles were no exception.
However, whenever the tower began to "distort" and return to the white fog, the black eagles would flap their wings and leave the tower for a short time. The tower would then return to its original state.
This action was repeated over and over again, allowing the black eagles to stand tall on the tower.
"This battle is coming to an end …" The black eagle on the far right asked.
The Bird Spirit Gate once had four great pavilions. These black eagles were from the Ancient Moon Pavilion.
"Giving up Fog City is not a big deal. At worst, we can take it back after a while," the leading black eagle said coldly.
This black eagle had three purple feathers on its head. It was the Pavilion Master of the Ancient Moon Pavilion, Pure Fox.
"But if we give up Fog City, we will have to give up all the advantages we gained in the Wine Pool," a black shadow on the side said.
Pure Fox's eagle eyes revealed a cold light, "They lost in the Wine Pool once. They will still lose if they come back. Don't worry …"
Just as Pure Fox was speaking, a small bird flew over.
The small bird came in front of the black eagles and chanted something …
The black eagles listened carefully.
After a short while, their eyes lit up and their faces lit up with joy.
"What?"
"They retreated?"
"They all retreated from Fog City?"
The black eagles' faces were filled with disbelief.
The snake masters had almost taken over Fog City. The Ancient Moon Pavilion's troops were already at the end of their rope.
Just as Pure Fox was about to give up Fog City, the snake masters retreated …
"Could it be a trap?" Pure Fox asked suspiciously.
"This Fog City can swallow everything left behind. They can't set up anything in Fog City. Once they retreat, they can only start over. What kind of trap could it be?" A black eagle shook its head.
"That's right! No matter what kind of trap they have, they will definitely give up Fog City! "Another black shadow agreed.
"Then let the birds patrol the entire Fog City!" Pure Fox ordered.
"Chirp chirp chirp …"
Soon, hundreds and thousands of small birds spread their wings and flew through the streets of Fog City, patrolling. As a result, they didn't catch a single snake's shadow.
After the news was reported, it was confirmed that the snake masters had retreated.
The other black eagles couldn't figure it out. They all looked at Pure Fox in the center.
Pure Fox's eyes were filled with suspicion and uneasiness.
If the snake masters occupied Fog City, Pure Fox's heart would be at ease.
Now that they retreated, it meant that there was something more important than Fog City …
Fog City could take back the wine pool. There was something more important than this …
"I'll go find the Sobbing Bird. It should be able to give me an answer!"
Pure Fox flapped her wings and flew away.
…
Mother World.
In the Lake Sound Continent.
Several black ships floated in the mirror-like lake.
In the battle of Li Mountain, the black ship's vitality was also greatly damaged, but it wasn't as bad as You Xiong and the Divine Farmer Clan.
Most of the crew survived.
But when Evil Deities was appointed as the new captain, the seeds of anger were planted in the hearts of the crew.
During this period of time, the crew took various opportunities to escape from the black ship.
According to the old rules of the black ship, these escaping crew members would be regarded as traitors and would be hunted down by the black ship.
But the old captain didn't treat them like this. Instead, he allowed the crew to leave. Now the black ship only existed in name.

Chapter 3650
As the captain of the ship, Evil Deities didn't care about the departure of the crew.
In the end, there were only two people left on the black ship.
One was the captain of the ship, while the other was Shen Tu.
Shen Tu opened the cabin door and looked towards the Evil God who was sitting at the stern, a smile spreading across her face as she walked over.
After approaching Evil Deities, she stretched out her pale and slender arm and wrapped it around Evil Deities' neck. "I brought you a gift from the other shore"
"Take a look" Evil Deities said.
Shen Tu began to take out her token, which was a finely crafted fish-mouth flask.
The Fish Mouth Flask was a good Paramita Token, but this Paramita Token obviously didn't match Shenshu.
"What is this token used for?" Evil Deities asked.
Although many tokens couldn't be seen at a glance, experienced people could tell from their aura.
Some of the mysterious Nirvana Realm tokens had very unique and powerful abilities at a glance, while some of the strengthening Nirvana Realm tokens exuded surging power.
"It's a container" Shen Tu blinked her round red eyes.
This time, it was Evil Deities' turn to be surprised.
"Is it really just a flask?" Evil Deities asked.
"That's right" Shen Tu smiled.
Strictly speaking, this fish-mouth flask was a space-type token, but it wasn't a powerful one.
Shen Tu was helping Tongtian deliver the thing inside the flask, so under normal circumstances, she wouldn't be able to fuse with this token.
"What's inside the flask?" Evil Deities asked again.
Shen Tu opened the flask and a pale yellow energy came out.
Evil Deities looked at this pale yellow energy and his pupils shrank, "Shinto energy?"
How sensitive was he to Shinto energy? He could tell almost without thinking, this was an unfamiliar Shinto energy!
"Yes, it's the Shinto energy that Yuan Ling civilization first extracted. It doesn't have a name yet, you can give it a name" Shen Tu suggested.
"Not interested"
The Evil God glanced at the Divine Dao energy floating in the air and took a deep breath. This Divine Dao energy drilled into his body through his nose, and he immediately pushed Shen Tu away.
"I'm going into seclusion, don't disturb me during this time" Evil Deities said.
Shen Tu's face showed a hint of displeasure, but she endured it and said, "Tongtian also brought a message."
"Speak!" Evil Deities said.
In Evil Deities' eyes, neither Shen Tu nor the previous captain was worth mentioning.
But Tongtian was still very useful to him...
"After fusing with this Shinto energy, You Xiong and the Divine Farmer Clan will be expelled from the Mother world" Shen Tu said.
The Evil God's eyes narrowed. "Order or request?"
Shen Tu was silent for a moment..
"Shen Tu's request" Shen Tu's's reply
Shen Tu's heart, Shen Tu's's's Deities's Ship...
In the cabin, the captain's face appeared on a shabby wooden board. The captain looked calm.
"From the first day you met him, you should have expected this, right?" The captain asked.
Shen Tu shook her head. "No."
"There's no need to lie to yourself. I know it, you know it, the disciples of the Sect Masters know it, and even Sect Master Tongtian himself knows it." The shipmaster looked up at the deck and said, "The evil god knows it himself."
The Evil God did not have any emotions. He would only be patient when he could make use of something.
In fact, Evil Deities had no interest in becoming the captain of the Kurofune. Evil Deities stayed on the Kurofune because of Tongtian.
"But we need him more. He's almost perfect," the captain said. "We'll do our best to help him, even if it means sacrificing ourselves."
If the Kurofune was a force supported by the Wu Kong Clan in the Chaotic World, then Evil Deities was the Wu Kong Clan's' 'big bet.
When Evil Deities obtained the last Shinto, they were undoubtedly very happy.
As soon as Shen Tu left, his figure flashed and he appeared in a cabin of the Kurofune.
He looked up at the wall.
Without thinking, he knew a pair of eyes were watching him from inside the wall.
"Watch if you like!"
Evil Deities laughed disdainfully.
He knew that the old captain was watching him all the time.
Then, strands of Shinto energy rushed out and began to converge.
Three thousand Shinto energies were fused one by one. Wanting them to not conflict with each other and have a stable arrangement, the difficulty could be imagined.
But in the hands of Evil Deities, these Shinto energies seemed to have souls and lives.
Each Shinto could quickly find a suitable target to fuse with …
Evil Deities had practiced this fusion process countless times.
Every additional energy was a test for Evil Deities. The last time they fused the three thousand and first Shinto, Evil Deities had a lot of headaches.
Now that they were fusing the last Shinto, it was a new challenge for Evil Deities!
"Weng …"
A colorful energy sphere floated in front of Evil Deities.
The three thousand and first Shinto energies formed a complete energy sphere. All the Shinto energies clung to each other, forming a delicate balance.
Once any Shinto was added or subtracted, the balance would be broken.
This "edifice" constructed from Shinto energies would collapse in an instant.
The Evil God carefully observed the sphere, trying to find a crack in it. However, it had spent so much effort to fuse the three thousand and one Divine Daos, so how could there be a crack in this sphere of Dao energy?
After thinking for a long time …
With a thought, he pulled out a Divine Dao from the Dao Fusion energy.
Evil Deities extracted the Five Elements Shinto.
As the most widespread Shinto energy, the Five Elements Shinto could be said to be the foundation of Shinto energy.
Once the Five Elements Shinto was extracted, the entire Shinto energy sphere would have to be reconstructed!
Evil Deities had an unparalleled talent on the path of Shinto energy. He only needed to observe for a while to make this judgment.
He had to do this!
After the Five Elements Shinto was extracted, the three thousand Shinto energies became chaotic, and a powerful energy aura spread out.
Seeing that the Shinto energy sphere was about to explode, Evil Deities reached out and continuously extracted and stuffed it in. These Shinto energies transformed into thin threads that weaved continuously within the sphere. In the end, the energy aura was forcibly suppressed by Evil Deities.
Within the wall, the old captain's eyes also saw this scene in its entirety.
He couldn't help but sigh in his heart …
This kind of talent and method of controlling energy was terrifyingly powerful. The old captain, who had seen countless people, sighed in amazement.
After Evil Deities stabilized the Shinto energy sphere, they restored the Five Elements Shinto energy once more.
On the surface, the Shinto energy didn't seem to have changed much, but in reality, the entire structure had undergone a tremendous change.
Now, there was a gap in the sphere …
This gap was left for the energy of the final Divine Dao.
"Weng!"
Evil Deities once again produced the Shinto energy sphere and slowly inserted it into the gap.
The two fused together almost perfectly …
Just as Evil Deities revealed a devilish smile, the Shinto energy sphere suddenly transformed into a snow-white sphere.
This sphere of light instantly annihilated the ship and bloomed on the surface of the lake.
The sphere of light was so bright that the entire world seemed as dark as night in front of it.

Chapter 3651
North of Jade Clear Sky.
There was a huge brown square stone erected in front of it.
This square stone was 29,000 feet long, 29,000 feet wide, and 29,000 feet tall.
From bottom to top, there was an opening every 30 feet.
The entire interior of the square stone was completely hollowed out, forming many intricate caves inside the square stone.
This place was known as Sky Square City, and it was also one of the four spirit sects' headquarters.
In a room on the upper left side of Sky Square City.
A man dressed in cotton clothes was tightly bound by four iron chains, and his limbs were pulled straight.
Hoo-Swish!
A white whip shook violently in the air and lashed towards the man in linen clothes.
Slap!
When the white whip lashed on the cotton clothed man, it released a layer of silver-white light. This white light was the Light of Annihilation.
The cotton clothed man also had the Treacherous Body.
Ordinary punishment methods would not be able to cause the Treacherous Body to feel pain. Using the Light of Annihilation to whip was like using thorns to whip the body.
Of course, the target had to be a Resentment Level or Hatred Level Treacherous Body.
If it was an ordinary Hatred Level Treacherous Body, this whip would directly turn them into ashes.
Slap! Slap! Slap!
Over the past few days, the cotton clothed man had been whipped countless times.
The one wielding the whip was a human woman. Every time she lashed out, she would ask, "Shen Chen Gang, do you know your crime?!"
Slap!
"Shen Chen Gang, do you know your crime?!"
Slap!
"Shen Chen Gang, do you know your crime?!"
The cotton clothed man's name was Shen Chen Gang. He was the Manor Lord of the Yue Residence under the Human Spirit Sect.
The human woman whipping Shen Chen Gang was also under the Yue Residence.
Although the woman's voice was stern, there was no pleasure in her eyes. Instead, it was filled with heartache.
The Yue Residence was in charge of guarding the Aquarius Stage. It was to guard that Divine Dao.
For so many primal chaos epochs, it had been peaceful, but now, it was snatched away by the Wu Kong Clan.
The Manor Lord's mistake was unforgivable.
Slap!
The woman had been whipping him for a few days and nights.
If she continued whipping him with the Light of Annihilation, she could really beat Shen Chen Gang to death.
Shen Chen Gang did not say a word. He only silently endured the pain.
During the continuous whipping, a voice transmitted over.
"General Chen Residence, have you enjoyed yourself these few days?"
That voice was filled with mockery.
The woman frowned when she heard the voice and increased the force of her whip by 30%.
The Manor Head was being punished. If she were to show favoritism, it would only cause Shen Chengang to be blamed even more.
It wasn't until the person outside the door walked in and made a hand gesture to the human woman, indicating for her to stop that she carefully kept her whip and stood aside.
The whip was filled with the light of destruction, so she naturally didn't dare to touch it.
The newcomer was a middle-aged man. He was none other than the Manor of Shifang's Manor.
Shen Chengang was the Yue Manor's Prefectural General, while the Yue Manor's Prefectural General was the Guang Manor's Prefectural General.
Regardless of whether it was before or after becoming a Shift, Shen Chengang had always been stronger than the Prefect of Chi. Moreover, the two of them had always been competitors.
After being revived as a Shift, the Ji Residence would be placed in an important position. Shen Chengang was actually being sidelined when he was sent to the Aquarius Stage.
Now that the Hou Family had come, they naturally had the intention of watching a joke.
Shen Chengang glanced at Si Fu. "You want to clear my coordinates?"
For Shift, as long as the coordinates were still there, it could revive indefinitely.
Apart from that, all other offensive methods against Shift were actually meaningless.
The human woman's continuous whipping only brought pain to Shen Chengang.
Many of the Yue Manor's people had been killed on the Aquarius Stage, but they would still revive at the coordinates in the blink of an eye.
Once the coordinates were cleared, it was equivalent to a death sentence. Shift would die forever.
The Prefect of Chi laughed. "It seems that the Shen Manor's General, who has always disregarded life and death, is also afraid of death?"
"When I was saved by those snakes, I was already prepared," Shen Chengang replied.
"Now is the time to use people. They won't kill you. Besides, using the Yue Manor to guard the Aquarius Stage is only the first layer of insurance. That Divine Dao energy itself is the second layer of insurance!" The Prefect of Chi stepped forward and said.
"What did they do?" Shen Chengang's gaze sharpened.
"Hurry up and release the Shen Manor's General!" The Prefect of Chi turned around and said to the human woman.
The human woman's expression lit up. She hurriedly stepped forward and untied the chains that bound the Shen Manor's General.
Then, the Prefect of Chi took out a mirror.
The image in the mirror was very blurry. One could only vaguely see the surface of a lake. There were a few black ships floating in the lake.
"What is this?" Shen Chengang guarded the Aquarius Stage all year round. His information was very limited.
"Black ships. A force supported by the birds in the Mother World," the Prefect of Chi said.
"That Divine Dao energy is in their hands?" Shen Chengang asked.
The Prefect of Chi nodded. "There is one person in the black ships who can fuse Divine Daos. They should be in a hurry to fuse Divine Dao energy perfectly."
"Perfect Divine Dao energy." Shen Chengang pursed his lips. "How can this be perfect?"
The definition of perfection was a limit.
The energy of a subworld could be increased or decreased at will for the Four Spirit Gates.
For example, the world's creator defined a subworld as having only a thousand types of energy. For this subworld, a thousand types of energy was the limit.
If there were three thousand types, then three thousand Divine Daos would be the limit.
"For the Chaosworld, this is perfection. Don't forget, you and I are now members of the Chaosworld. Don't use your previous standards to define it," the Prefect of Chi said.
It would be very difficult for them to return to that vast world.
Shen Chengang glanced at the Prefect of Chi. "If he grasps the complete Divine Dao energy, he might have the ability to change the structure of the Chaosworld. You don't seem to be worried at all?"
"If he fuses the last Divine Dao, the second insurance will naturally be activated. Let's wait and see," the Prefect of Chi said proudly.
The Prefect of Chi, Shen Chengang, and the human girl quietly observed the scene in the mirror.
Because the scene was too blurry, they could only see the blurry black ship and the surface of the lake. They could not see any other details.
However, several hours later.
The black ship in the center suddenly shook violently.
It was as if a giant threw a mountain into the lake, creating a huge wave.
Then, the black ship was covered by a white light.
"It's quite powerful."
The general patted Shen Chengang's shoulder and handed the mirror over to him. Then, he turned around and left.
Shen Chengang held the mirror and watched as the white light gradually dimmed and disappeared.
The black ship fell apart on the surface of the lake. At the same time, black blood gushed out.
The black ship was a part of the old captain's body. If it was damaged too severely, it would not be able to recover quickly. Naturally, the heavily injured old captain would lose a lot of blood.
"They suspect that I purposely released the Divine Dao energy to Tongtian," Shen Chengang said.
The human girl beside him nodded. "Did you send this mirror here to warn us?" "It's not a warning." Shen Chengang squeezed the mirror and shattered it. "It's a disdainful mockery. It wants to tell us that any tricks are meaningless!"

Chapter 3652
There were hundreds of thousands of lakes of various sizes in Huming Province.
The Clear Sky Lake in the center was the largest. Looking down from above, it looked like a magnificent jewel embedded in the horizon.
When a bright ball of light suddenly rose from Huming Province, all the creatures in Huming Province were stunned.
They stared at the ball of light in the sky, not knowing what was happening.
A few breaths later.
The terrifying shockwave reached them.
Along with the shockwave, waves of water swept up to thousands of meters high.
With Clear Sky Lake as the center, everything within tens of thousands of miles was swept up by the waves.
After two hours, the lake finally returned to normal.
The Clear Sky Lake's water level dropped by more than eighty thousand feet, leaving behind only a pool of water.
Three black ships were lying on the "pool of water", their hulls were covered with crisscrossing cracks. Blood flowed out from the splints of the ships from time to time.
Along with the blood flowing out, the old captain's pained moans could be heard.
Over the years, the black ships had suffered a lot of damage. A few of them were even completely crushed and burned.
But that was when the old captain was fully prepared.
Destroying one or two black ships was like cutting off an arm or a leg. With the old captain's Immortal realm cultivation, it was not a problem at all.
The black ship that was now docked in Huming Province was his only remaining body!
The black ships were severely damaged at the same time, and the black ship that carried the Evil God was smashed to pieces.
For the old captain, this was a very serious injury.
When the black ship exploded, Shen Tu and Evil Deities were on the same ship.
Fortunately, the old captain sensed that something was wrong and directly ejected Shen Tu out of the cabin.
Even so, Shen Tu was heavily smashed into the side of the black ship by the shockwave. His entire body was embedded in the deck, many parts of his body were torn apart.
"Crack, crack, crack"
When Shen Tu woke up, he heard the sound of the black ship being reassembled.
The old captain was desperately trying to repair the black ship.
If an opponent came now, the old captain wouldn't even have the strength to escape.
"Captain," Shen Tu called out after waking up, "What happened?"
Not long after the battle of Li Mountain.
Logically speaking, the Mother World should be experiencing a period of peace.
Whether it was Li Mountain or You Xiong Clan, it was impossible for them to start another war.
That scale of energy just now couldn't be …
Shen Tu had just come to this conclusion when the old captain said, "It's Dao Fusion energy."
"Dao Fusion energy? How is that possible!? "Shen Tu couldn't believe his eyes.
If the Evil Deities were to fuse with the Dao, they wouldn't use too much energy. Although the Dao Fusion energy was very unstable and extraordinarily powerful, if there was too little energy, no matter how powerful it was, it wouldn't be able to cause so much damage.
"I saw it with my own eyes," the old captain said with certainty.
"The Evil Deities …" Shen Tu's pupils trembled slightly.
"I don't know."
When the white ball of light exploded, it completely devoured the body of the Evil Deities.
The old captain had no time to save the Evil Deities. He couldn't even protect himself.
Shen Tu struggled to his feet and floated up into the air, taking a few steps across the lake before quickly spreading out his Divine Sense.
The surface area of the lake had shrunk countless times, allowing Shen Tu's Divine Sense to easily cover it.
She couldn't find any traces of Evil Deities within the range of her Divine Sense.
"Did he disappear?" Shen Tu murmured.
"That energy was so powerful, he might have directly evaporated," the old captain said.
With such terrifying energy, it was simply too easy to destroy a person.
Now that she couldn't find the Evil Deities, the biggest possibility was that he had directly evaporated, leaving nothing behind.
"He couldn't have died like this!" Shen Tu was a little excited.
The old captain hesitated for a moment before saying, "Let's wait and see."
The Wu Kong Clan had invested a lot in the Evil Deities. If the Evil Deities died like this, it would be a great loss for the Wu Kong Clan.
Just as Shen Tu continued to float in the air and was about to expand her search range, she suddenly felt a force in front of her.
She reflexively wanted to avoid it, but the force was fierce. In the blink of an eye, it turned into a big hand that grabbed her, and then a huge figure appeared in front of her.
It was the Evil Deities.
"Evil ship, Captain! You're fine! "Shen Tu said in surprise.
"How could I die like this?" The Evil Deities sneered.
He lifted Shen Tu up and put her on his shoulder, then looked up at the sky.
The Evil Deities had an extraordinary perception ability in the Mother World.
He could perceive that there was something strange in the sky above Huming Province. There were two faintly discernible passages floating there, and someone was spying down through them.
"What's in the sky?" Shen Tu asked.
"Someone is observing me," The Evil Deities replied.
"Who is it?" Shen Tu frowned.
"I don't know," The Evil Deities said calmly, "And it's not just one, it seems there are two."
Saying so, he stretched out his hand.
An energy spiral formed in front of his fist.
"Hua!"
The energy spiral was like a giant dragon soaring into the sky. It drilled into one of the passageways and pulverized the passageway.
Inside Clear Jade Heaven.
Shen Chen Gang crushed the mirror, and the fragments splashed all over the ground.
Just as Shen Chen Gang was about to leave, the human woman exclaimed, "Palace Master, look!"
The image on the mirror was originally extremely blurry.
Now that it had been broken into hundreds of pieces, it was even harder to see.
Shen Chen Gang reached out and gently touched the mirror fragments, and the hundreds of fragments quickly pieced together as if they had a soul, becoming a whole.
As soon as the mirror was pieced together, the human woman said, "That person isn't dead, he seems to have discovered the snake masters' observation passage, what a sharp guy"
"He wants to destroy the passage," Shen Chen Gang said.
"Boom!"
The next thing they saw was the Evil Deities throwing a punch.
Nothing happened after the punch, and although the image on the mirror was blurry, it still existed.
"The punch missed?" The human woman asked.
Shen Chen Gang stared at the mirror for a while, then shook his head, "It doesn't look like it missed, he broke another passage. Besides the snake masters' observation passage, there should be another observation passage"
"Another observation passage? Those birds are also observing him? "The human woman asked.
"That's unlikely," Shen Chen Gang shook his head again.
The birds controlled the Wu Kong clan, and the Wu Kong clan controlled the black ship.
Now that Evil Deities and the black ship were a whole, it was easier to observe Evil Deities through the black ship, and there was no need to be afraid.
"But other than us and the birds, who else would be observing him?" The human woman felt strange.
There was a third force in the Nirvana Realm?
"We don't know yet."
Just as Shen Chengang was speaking, the Evil God's fist came smashing down again.
An energy spiral soared into the sky, and finally destroyed the snake masters' observation passage.
In Sky City, the images in more than twenty mirrors of various sizes disappeared at the same time.
Shen Chen Gang looked at the blank mirror, and there was a trace of inexplicable joy in the depth of his eyes, mixed with a hint of pleasure.

Chapter 3655
Below the big bell.
Hua Tianming slowly opened his eyes.
The Divine Clock City had built a huge plaza around the bell.
Beside the bell, there were dozens of people in a long line. They were all waiting to ring the bell.
Some were True God Great Perfection, hoping to ring the bell and enter the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda.
Some were in the Nirvana Realm, hoping to ring the bell and enter the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda.
To these geniuses of the Divine Realm, accidentally falling into the Sinkhole Graveyard was the best opportunity for them!
According to the past development of the Divine Territory, there would always be that many wealthy families in the Time Sea, and the number of Saints would be fixed.
They could only reach the True God Great Perfection in their lifetime, unless they defeated the other wealthy families.
But after the Luo Family took control of the Divine Territory, such chances were slim.
After entering the Ultimate Graveyard, they had the True Meaning of Dao for themselves to cultivate, as well as a place to help them cultivate it. Naturally, they had the foundation to reach the Nirvana Realm.
According to the rules, the Nirvana Token obtained from the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda had to be displayed.
Everyone's eyes were filled with anticipation when they saw Hua Tianming wake up.
"Tianming! What did you get? "a child-like person asked.
This person was Luo Zheng's senior brother, Jing Wuhuan.
Now, Jing Wuhuan had obtained a level 15 Nirvana Token. Although he was not as monstrous as Hua Tianming, he was still one of the best.
"Is it the Wings of Whirlwind?" a youth beside Jing Wuhuan asked.
This youth was Luo Zheng's second senior brother, Yu Shenfeng. His luck and talent were better than Jing Wuhuan's. Now that he had obtained a level 19 Nirvana Token, he could be ranked in the top three of the Nirvana Realm in the Divine Territory.
"Nightmare Stone." Hua Tianming took out the Nirvana Token that had yet to be carried.
Seeing the gray stone, everyone was in an uproar.
Although this stone was one of the best Nirvana Tokens among the 31 levels, it was definitely not suitable for Hua Tianming.
"Why?" Jing Wuhuan asked curiously.
Even if he could not obtain the Wings of Whirlwind and the Hallowed Battle Sword, Hua Tianming did not need to choose such a stone.
Hua Tianming was also confused. "I took the Wings of Whirlwind, but it disappeared …"
"Disappear …" Yu Shen Feng had a confused look on his face.
"Something like this has happened before." Jing Wuhuan's child-like face revealed an expression that did not match his appearance. "The Mysterious Yellow Pagoda of Heaven and Earth is guiding you. It does not want you to take away the two Nirvana tokens. The Nightmare Stone may be the most important to you."
The people in the Sinkhole Cemetery did not know much about the Heaven Earth Mystic Yellow Pagoda, but they knew that strange things would occasionally happen in the Heaven Earth Mystic Yellow Pagoda.
Leading them to choose the Nirvana Token was one of them.
"But the function of this stone doesn't meet my needs," Hua Tianming said with a frown.
Jing Wuhuan smiled and said, "The Mysterious Yellow Pagoda understands you better than you do. Furthermore, we don't have a complete understanding of the Nirvana Tokens in the upper levels of the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda. We can only speculate about their uses. This Nightmare Stone might not be a strengthening Nirvana Token.
Things. "
The ability of a Nirvana Realm Token was very difficult to confirm. It could only be confirmed after it was carried.
There were only a few bearers of a high-level Nirvana Realm token like the thirty-first step. The analysis of it was just speculation.
"The results of the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda's guidance every time are very good!" Yu Shenfeng interrupted from the side.
In the past, Yu Shenfeng had little interest in the path of cultivation. After entering the Nirvana Realm, he had done his homework. This was also the reason why Yu Shenfeng had advanced so quickly.
Hearing what the two of them said, Hua Tianming, who was originally a little disappointed, became excited. He wanted to see what was so magical about this Nightmare Stone.
"Thank you for your auspicious words, Seniors!" Hua Tianming cupped his hands at the two of them.
"Dong —"
At this moment, the big bell in the middle of the square rang again.
The person who rang the big bell was at Nirvana Realm Level Five. The bell only rang for about ten breaths.
After that person opened his eyes, he showed the Nirvana Token he had obtained and left the big bell.
"It's my turn!"
Yu Shenfeng said confidently and walked toward the big bell.
"Senior Shen Feng is very confident," Hua Tianming said with a smile.
Jing Wuhuan sighed and said, "He has benefited greatly from the Dripping Stone. This time, he plans to ring the bell twice."
"Twice in a row …" Hua Tianming was stunned.
Other than shock, the others' eyes were also filled with envy.
Dripping Stone …
It was really a magical place for cultivation.
It was a huge stone cave.
The climate in the stone cave was cold and humid. There were hundreds of caves of various sizes inside.
These caves had "clean water" dripping down all year round. These sparkling and translucent water droplets contained a unique aura that could greatly increase one's comprehension of the True Intent of the Dao. This place was originally used to comprehend the True Intent of the Dao.
There were many places in the Ultimate cemetery to comprehend the True Meaning of Tao, such as the Sword Grave, Nature's Gate, and so on. Each place corresponded to a True Meaning of Tao.
The Dripping Stone was not targeted and its effects were ordinary, so it was not popular.
They would only consider entering the Dripping Stone when the Sword Grave and Nature's Gate were full.
Not long after the Deity Realm merged into the Ultimate Cemetery, there was a change in the stone cave. The clean water that dripped from the cave was mixed with turbid water.
The resources in the Ultimate Cemetery had to be competed for. As a new superpower, the Deity Realm found it hard to squeeze into the popular cultivation grounds. Hence, the relatively unpopular Dripping Stone became their first choice.
Hua Tianming, Yu Shenfeng, Jing Wuhuan, Shi Xiaoqiao, and the other Grand Perfection experts were the first batch of Deity Realm humans to enter the Dripping Stone.
When the turbid water poured into their bodies, their physiques rapidly grew!
The Ultimate Cemetery did not have the 'Chaotic Origin Great World' or the 'Land of Calamity Bones'. Hence, it was hard to solve the problem of the fleshy body. Hence, after Gu Bei reached the Paramita Realm, it was hard for him to improve his cultivation.
Naturally, it became a burden.
The transformation of the Dripping Stone caused a huge commotion in the Ultimate Cemetery.
That time, for the sake of the location of the cave within the 'Dripping Stone', a battle broke out in the Ultimate Cemetery.
Gu Bei represented the human side and reserved a considerable number of spots for the Deity Realm.
Because of the transformation of the Dripping Stone, the Deity Realm humans and the other races in the Ultimate Cemetery were able to produce a batch of Paramita Realm experts in a short period of time!
In comparison, the Sages of the Deity Realm were much quieter …
Even though the Sages were top-notch geniuses, they similarly entered the Dripping Stone to cultivate.
The strange thing was that no matter how hard the Sages worked, their cultivation speed was far slower than those Grand Perfection experts.
Other than Gu Bei, the ones who improved the fastest were Sacred Emperor Leng Yao of the Leng Clan and Secondary Sage Han Jiu Yi of the Han Clan. They had already cultivated to the 14th level of the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda.
As for Luo Xiao, Fang Shi Xing, Tang Lun, Jiu Sheng, and other veteran Sacred Emperors, they remained at the seventh and eighth level of the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda.
If they were placed in the true Paramita Realm, they would be existences that even the 13 Heavens of the Color Realm could not surpass.

Chapter 3679
Once the Thirteen Ultimate Teeth Chain was pulled off, the Monkey King's body immediately regained its freedom.
But the Monkey King was still in an unawakened state, and the "dream" firmly bound its own consciousness.
Once the wolf fangs were removed, the Monkey King's ability to control itself was greatly weakened.
But that didn't mean it couldn't resist.
No matter what happened outside, it was frantically venting its power.
Sometimes it rolled, sometimes it fell, and sometimes it waved its fists like a madman.
The bifid serpent tried to retrieve the wolf fangs that fell into the lake.
It was unwilling to fail like this …
But now the Monkey King had become extremely dangerous.
Any living being that lingered around it would be attacked indiscriminately.
The bifid serpent and the Eye Spirit had tried several times but failed. They didn't even have the chance to search for the wolf fangs in the lake.
Seeing that this mission was a complete failure, the bifid serpent gritted its teeth. "Let's go!"
"Whoosh!"
It turned into a white shadow and drilled into the center of the lake.
The Eye Spirit, Fire, and Claire also followed closely behind and drilled into the center of the lake.
While they drilled into the center of the lake, several monkey masters followed closely behind.
The monkey masters weren't chasing after the bifid serpent.
Now that the Monkey King had suffered such a big change, they were not in the mood to chase after it, let alone kill the bifid serpent. It could still be resurrected at the coordinates.
Their purpose was to expel the bifid serpent and prevent it from destroying the channel.
The channel of the Ghost Mountain had been repaired with great difficulty. How could they let outsiders destroy it casually?
But just now, the monkey masters did consider destroying the channel to prevent the Monkey King's body from being taken away.
But that was the last choice …
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
The Monkey King was still struggling.
It was venting its power in the central lake.
No one dared to approach it, and no one dared to come forward to comfort it.
The Monkey King's power was almost infinite, and no one knew how long this situation would last.
Luo Zheng and the others floated on the lake and looked at each other from a distance, waiting silently.
The three monkey lords flew over.
These three were Kongu, Summer, and Inch.
Their status in the Monkey Sect was second only to Dagu.
"Monkey Masters, what can I do for you?" The Honoured Lord of the Origin stepped forward and bowed.
Kong Gu glanced at Fuxi's original body and said, "You're good. You've kept our king here!"
"Why didn't you choose to escape during the chaos? Why did you take such a huge risk to save our king?" Xia Gu asked.
The hearts of these monkey lords were as clear as a mirror.
Even though he had left these humans in Ghost Mountain for countless years, they were not the same kind of people after all. There was no affection to speak of.
This was the case for the monkey lords, and it was the same for the humans.
"Because we have a favor to ask of the Monkey Spirit King and the entire Monkey Spirit Sect," the Primeval Lord of Heaven replied.
"You want us to stand behind you humans?" Ancient Inch asked.
"Yes!"
There was no room for negotiation with such a request, and the Heavenly Lord of the Origin did not do anything. He was telling the truth.
"We can't make the decision." Kong Gu shook his head.
"Big Gu can't make the decision either," said Xia Gu.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven smiled. "I know. Only the Ghost Lord can make the decision."
"Yes, if it cannot awaken, your request cannot be satisfied," Ancient Inch replied.
"What if I can awaken the Ghost Lord?" asked the Primeval Lord of Heaven.
When the Primeval Lord of Heaven said this, the eyes of the three monkey lords lit up.
They were clear about the Primeval Lord of Heaven's nature.
If he was not confident, he would not say such a thing.
Since he said so, there was a possibility …
"Can you do it?" Kong Gu asked.
"Perhaps," the Primeval Lord of Heaven replied.
"Very good." Kong Gu continued, "If you can awaken our king, all of us monkey lords will do our best to help you."
The monkey lords were indeed unable to make a decision, but the Monkey Spirit King would take their suggestions into consideration.
This was what the Primeval Lord of Heaven wanted. This was the highest promise that the monkey lords could give.
"You must collect all the wolf fangs," said the Primeval Lord of Heaven.
"And then?" Xia Gu asked.
He wanted to know where the Primeval Lord of Heaven's confidence came from.
"Leave the rest to us," the Primeval Lord of Heaven said with a serious expression.
The secret of the Origin God Blood could not be casually revealed, even to the Monkey Spirit Sect.
Kong Gu, Xia Gu, and Ancient Inch did not ask any more questions.
They plunged into the water and began to search for the wolf fangs.
The wolf fangs had become very large, so it was not difficult to find them.
The Monkey Spirit King gradually moved to the north of Night Vision Island. Without the Monkey Spirit King's obstruction, the thirteen wolf fangs were all collected in less than half an hour.
…
The Spirit Monkey King's crazed state lasted for a longer time than everyone had expected.
It lasted for ten days and ten nights!
Fortunately, most of its range of activity was in the water. Only the northern part of the crater was destroyed.
In the end, the Monkey Spirit King gradually calmed down.
The monkey lords did not think of a way to move the Monkey Spirit King, so they let the Monkey Spirit King sit in the lake.
During these ten days, some people were happy while others were sad.
After the Ghost Mountain was destroyed, many Foe Level and Hatred Level monsters escaped. These were captured by the monkey lords and the Grand Ghost Sacrificial Ghost.
The Crimson Demons, Dami Demons, and Kite Demons who were originally waiting at the Ghost Sacrificial Altar also secretly escaped to their own clans.
When the monkey lords were fighting with the Two-headed Serpent, they also secretly observed and roughly guessed what had happened.
The two clan leaders, Tu Ye and Mi Gui, were extremely anxious every day.
If they stayed in Night Vision Island, they would not be able to escape death.
But the passage to Night Vision Island was guarded by the monkey lords. It was impossible for them to escape.
These days, they could only quietly wait for death to come.
In contrast, the atmosphere in the Kite Demon Clan was much more relaxed.
Although Night Vision Island had undergone earth-shaking changes, and all the foreign clans were afraid that they would face unpredictable changes, they chose to follow the human race.
Cang Duo was originally guarded by a Grand Ghost Sacrificial Ghost.
As soon as the Ghost Mountain was destroyed, Ma Yuen did not care about Cang Duo. He also left the Ghost Mountain in confusion and walked in the direction of Konoha.
This time, he entered the Ghost Mountain just to try his luck. Maybe he could fuse with a powerful Foe Level monster.
He did not even dare to think about a Resentment Level monster …
In the end, he got a Hatred Level monster.
Tiger Thorn was still in its glass piece.
Cang Duo did not dare to try to fuse with Tiger Thorn, but he still had the courage to carefully communicate with Tiger Thorn.
Tiger Thorn was a kind species and did not reject Cang Duo.
After a few days of communication, they got along more and more well.
Cang Duo's heart was filled with excitement!
If the Patriarch saw a Hatred Level monster, what would his expression be?
Even if he did not fuse with it, he would have unparalleled combat power!
Thinking of this, its footsteps became lighter as it went home. However, when he returned to Konoha's territory, his face suddenly changed. A group of Evil Tiger warriors were surrounding the Konoha people halfway up the mountain.

Chapter 3693
On the huge pillar, Luo Nian was climbing up the stairs of pages step by step.
Climbing this huge pillar was a very tedious task.
Every one thousand steps, there would be a statue blocking the way.
It was fine if he encountered those rotten statues, but he just had to cross them carefully.
As for the statues that were still intact and could move, they were a big problem.
Every time Luo Nian encountered a statue that could move, he would pull the rope hard.
Jiang Ziya, who was under the pillar, would pull Luo Nian down from the pillar.
Although Jiang Ziya could not see clearly, he could not see the situation on the pillar.
But he was very good at controlling his strength.
The rope was shrinking rapidly, dragging Luo Nian all the way down. When he reached the ground, Jiang Ziya stretched out his hand and caught Luo Nian firmly.
Then Luo Nian patted Jiang Ziya's shoulder gently, and the two of them ran toward the nearest cave.
Once these statues locked onto their target, they would not give up.
They spiraled down the stairs of pages and fell all the way into the cave, chasing Luo Nian into the nearest world.
After the previous few battles, Jiang Ziya realized that this would be a long battle, so he set up a big formation at the entrance of this world.
Luo Zheng and Jiang Ziya entered first.
After the statues entered, the big formation was activated quickly.
Bolts of golden lightning shot out from all directions of the formation, striking the weak points of the statue.
"Pa, pa, pa …"
The statues were burnt by the lightning, and Jiang Ziya took out the Holy Whip.
Thousands of golden whip shadows hit the statues, and the sturdy statues immediately fell apart.
Jiang Ziya sighed after destroying the statues, "Fortunately, these statues have been greatly corroded by time."
The statues used to be much more powerful than this, at least they were not something that Jiang Ziya could resist.
At this moment, Jiang Ziya noticed Luo Nian's expression. "Nian 'er, what's wrong?"
"I'm tired." Luo Nian curled his lips.
Luo Nian's mind was not tough.
He came to explore the four pillars purely out of interest.
At first, he thought it was very interesting …
He could control other people's Nirvana Token at will and climb up layer by layer. What would he encounter if he climbed to the top of the pillar?
It would be great if he could find his father's Nirvana Token. Luo Nian would definitely exchange it for a more powerful one for Luo Zheng. He wondered what kind of expression Luo Zheng would have when he found out that his Nirvana Token had been changed.
However, reality was always very different from imagination.
This pillar was too tall, too tall. It was unimaginably tall.
At first, Luo Nian counted the number of pages he had climbed.
Two thousand steps, five thousand steps, ten thousand steps, fifty thousand steps...
However, after every one or two thousand steps, he would always encounter those statues that were still moving.
He couldn't defeat the statues, so he could only pull on the rope and drop down from the sky. After dealing with the statues, he climbed up again.
The higher one climbed, the longer it would take to restart.
There were even a few times when he accidentally pulled the rope and was dragged down by Jiang Ziya.
It was so boring to do this over and over again.
Luo Nian, who had lost his patience, began to resist.
How could Jiang Ziya not see it?
If it were before, Jiang Ziya might have taken Luo Nian back first to make a long-term plan.
However, this pillar controlled the Nirvana Token in the Chaotic World!
Its importance was self-evident!
"We have been out for quite a long time this time," Jiang Ziya sighed. "It's been hard on you, Nian 'er, but..."
"I know," Luo Nian interrupted Jiang Ziya.
Then he grabbed the rope on the ground and walked out without saying a word.
Jiang Ziya revealed a faint smile.
Luo Nian had been doing things according to his temperament all these years. He rarely bore any responsibility.
This was a kind of training for Luo Nian.
He climbed again and again and fell again.
Luo Nian gradually learned to be patient.
The process of climbing the pillar wasn't completely boring.
These pages were connected to many Nirvana Tokens. Through the silk threads on the pages, he could spy on all aspects of the Nirvana Realm.
Once, Luo Nian saw a group of humans through the light.
He tried to communicate with them, but failed...
These lights could only project the scene of the destination, but could not help him transmit sound.
What Luo Nian could do was to change the light of the Nirvana Token to help them.
There were too many Nirvana Tokens...
A grain of sand, a piece of leaf, a stone...
Not to mention that there were so many creatures in the Nirvana Realm. It was unrealistic to check if the Nirvana Token belonged to Luo Zheng one by one.
Therefore, after a period of time, Luo Nian was too lazy to check the Nirvana Tokens. He just kept rushing upward.
When Luo Nian climbed to a certain height, he suddenly found that the statues guarding the pillar were almost decayed. They no longer chased after him.
This made Luo Nian more motivated, and his climbing speed also steadily increased.
Several months passed.
Luo Nian had climbed to a considerable height.
He poked his head out of the page and looked up the stairs. He could see the top of the pillar.
He looked down again.
Other than a lone rope, he couldn't see anything else. This place was too high.
The only connection between him and Jiang Ziya was this rope.
This rope should be very heavy after such a long distance. Why didn't it increase in weight?
A strange idea came to his mind.
He looked down the rope carefully, and his face changed slightly. He saw that the middle of the rope had been broken!
Luo Nian had no idea when it was broken.
Should he go down?
As soon as this idea came to his mind, he shook his head hard.
Although Luo Nian was annoyed, he had worked so hard for so long. If he retreated now, all his previous efforts would be wasted.
No matter what, he had to climb to the top of the pillar to have a look!
Therefore, without Jiang Ziya's protection, Luo Nian continued to climb up the page stairs.
On the upper half of the pillar, the light in the page stairs gradually became sparse.
Previously, each page stairs was densely distributed with light. Now, each page stairs only had one or two light rays.
Luo Nian could naturally see that the end of these light rays was related to an extraordinary Nirvana Token, but he did not stop.
After climbing for a few more days, he was very close to the top of the pillar. At this time, the page stairs under his feet suddenly changed color.
The gray page quickly gave out a burst of bright red!
And this red page had no light!
"No light means no Nirvana Token," Luo Nian murmured.

Chapter 3706
The commotion caused by the battle above Moon Shield City was too small, so small that the deaths of two Saint Soul Realm cultivators did not alarm the people of Moon Shield City.
They did not know that two Saint Soul Realm cultivators had just been killed.
Evil Deities slowly landed in the prison in the north of the city.
The guards of the prison swarmed out and rushed towards Evil Deities.
In the eyes of Evil Deities, it was as if these guards did not exist. Without seeing him do anything, countless stalagmites as thick as a person's wrist suddenly drilled out from the ground and skewered the guards.
The walls and fences of the prison suddenly collapsed, frightening the captives.
The captives did not dare to move and just stared blankly at Evil Deities descending from the sky.
Only Xiuzhu had a smile on her face as she slowly walked towards Evil Deities.
Xiuzhu was very worried and did not want Evil Deities to come personally. But since her husband was here, all her wishes were fulfilled.
Without asking or being surprised, she went into Evil Deities' arms without saying a word.
"Let's go," Evil Deities said.
Then he hugged Xiuzhu tightly and the two of them flew into the sky and landed on the deck of the black ship.
The moment Xiuzhu's feet touched the deck, she felt a burning gaze.
There was a hint of jealousy in that gaze, and even a hint of killing intent.
Xiuzhu and Shen Tu's eyes met. She first greeted Shen Tu, "Hello, Elder Sister," in a natural and graceful manner.
Shen Tu wanted to say something, but she did not know how to respond.
When she saw Xiuzhu leaning on Evil Deities so naturally and harmoniously, her heart sank.
This man came all the way to the Blood Sea Province just to save this woman. What else could she say?
However, Shen Tu was an old monster who had walked through mountains of corpses and seas of blood. She forced a smile and said, "Who are you to him?
Why are you in the Blood Sea Province? "
"She is my wife. She also came from Luo Zheng's inner world. Not long ago, Luo Zheng released her, but she was captured and brought here by the You Xiong Clan," answered the Evil Deities.
"So that's how it is," Shen Tu's eyes trembled slightly, "Since we've rescued her, can we go now?"
According to Shen Tu and the old captain's predictions, shortly after they entered the You Xiong Clan's ancestral land, Ji Xuanyuan should have personally taken action, but Ji Xuanyuan hadn't shown up for so long, so the situation was a bit strange.
Since the enemy didn't make any move, Kurofune didn't need to take the initiative to look for trouble. Leaving the territory of You Xiong Family now was the best choice.
"No." Evil Deities shook her head. "Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno are coming soon. I want to challenge them."
Hearing this, Shen Tu and the old captain not far away both frowned slightly.
Along the way, the old captain also reported the Evil Deities to the Prime of Tongtian.
Challenging Ji Xuanyuan would involve too many people, so he had to ask for the Prime of Tongtian's opinion.
The Prime of Tongtian didn't want to stop the Evil Deities. Instead, he wanted to evaluate the true strength of Evil Deities.
However, the Prime of Tongtian told him that Evil Deities would face Ji Xuanyuan alone.
If Ji Xuanyuan had Shinno by his side, the situation would be completely different.
Xiuzhu had heard of the great names of Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno.
In the Heart Flow Sword Sect, Xiuzhu also inquired about the information of the powerhouses in the Mother World.
Both of them were Immortal Realm powerhouses.
The window to reach the Immortal Realm had passed, so it was impossible for her husband to step into the Immortal Realm.
At this time, she was no longer worried about Evil Deities. She chose to trust her husband without reservation.
"Are you confident?"
The old captain asked, "Shen Tu and I can help you pin down one of them."
Although the people of Kurofune were either dead or scattered, they still had the old captain, who was an Immortal Realm powerhouse, and Shen Tu, who was at the peak of the Saint Soul Realm.
"You don't need to do anything. They're already here." Evil Deities suddenly turned to look at the space on the side of the ship. "They must have heard my words."
Weng …
In a unique triangular space, Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno's figures floated.
This triangular space completely sealed their auras, so the old captain and Shen Tu didn't notice them even though they were close by.
"The younger generation will surpass us in time. A little brat who hasn't even reached the Saint Soul Realm dares to challenge the two of us," Ji Xuanyuan said calmly.
Evil Deities' lips curled slightly, "I'm a little curious. In just a few days, you seem to have become a different person. Is that blood control secret technique of yours so useful?"
Hearing Evil Deities mention "blood control secret technique", Ji Xuanyuan's face sank slightly, "How do you know?"
Evil Deities looked at him with deep eyes, as if they could see through everything in the world, "The Mother World doesn't have many secrets to me."
Ji Xuanyuan was depressed.
This ability was too terrifying!
Back then, they had worked so hard to force out Gui Qing, but it was all for this person!
But when Ji Xuanyuan thought about it again, his heart thumped.
In fact, Gui Qing couldn't control the Mother World to this extent.
Evil Deities was obviously many times more powerful than Gui Qing when it came to fusing with the Mother World.
If he could control Evil Deities or find a way to devour him, it would be a great opportunity for Ji Xuanyuan!
"Very well, since you want to challenge us, then we'll fight to the death today!"
Ji Xuanyuan said and called out to the sky. "Sky!"
A behemoth suddenly appeared in the sky above the Blood Sea Region.
The people in Moon Shield City suddenly exclaimed.
The appearance of a behemoth like Sky wasn't a good thing. If those top powerhouses fought, the entire Blood Sea Region would suffer.
"Sky is here too!"
Shen Tu and the old captain suddenly felt bad.
Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno were troublesome enough. With the addition of a Primal Chaos Ancient God, the Kurofune had no chance of winning.
The old captain's first thought was to have Tongtian descend again!
"This place is restricted! Come to my Illusory Void! "
As soon as Sky's voice fell, a triangular door appeared in front of Evil Deities.
This was Sky's invitation to challenge.
"You are all famous powerhouses in the Primal Chaos. It's ridiculous that you want to fight one against three! Evil Deities, don't be fooled by them! "
Shen Tu stopped them.
Evil Deities looked at the triangular door and reached their hands into it to sense it. They said to Xiuzhu, "Wait for me", and then went in directly!
"Buzz, buzz, buzz!"
The next moment, the triangular door suddenly closed.
Ji Xuanyuan, Shinno, and Sky also quickly disappeared.
The people in Moon Shield City saw this scene and breathed a sigh of relief. They didn't want to be affected by such a destructive power.
"They really went in! What do we do now? "
Shen Tu asked anxiously.
The old captain didn't speak. He quickly sat down cross-legged and began to recite the True Meaning of Dao. He wanted to enter the other side again to communicate with Tongtian.
Looking at the panicked Shen Tu, Xiuzhu comforted him, "Husband will be fine. He will win."
"What do you know!"
Shen Tu looked at her arrogantly.
How could a puny True God understand the battle between top powerhouses?
