Chapter 3712
There seemed to be a waterfall of blood flowing down from the top of Mount Li.
If one looked closely, they would discover that these "blood waterfalls" were pure energy.
These blood waterfalls flowed from the top of the mountain all the way to the middle of the mountain, dyeing it red.
When the sect master and disciples of Mount Li saw these blood waterfalls, they were all shocked.
"If I'm not wrong, this is Goddess Nuwa's bloodline ability …"
"It's very similar to Nuwa's aura."
"But it's even purer and stronger than Nuwa's aura!"
After reaching the Immortal realm, Goddess Nuwa's strength had reached the peak.
Over the years, apart from changing a few top-tier Paramita tokens, her strength had not improved much.
Now that everyone felt Nuwa's strength, they were naturally shocked. It seemed that Goddess Nuwa had gained a lot in the Paramita realm.
"Whoosh!"
All the blood waterfalls gathered together, forming a huge Nuwa shadow that floated above Mount Li.
"Whoosh!"
Nuwa flew into the sky from the Jade Platform.
As she floated in the air, the powerful airflow caused her hair to flutter. Her eyes were filled with seriousness.
After mastering the complete Blood Control Secret Art, Nuwa couldn't wait to give it a try.
On the first day she returned to Mount Li, she pushed her bloodline ability to the limit.
"Mo, are you ready?" Nuwa asked.
"Weng …"
A vast power floated out from within Mount Li. It was the power of truth.
When the power of truth surged out of Mount Li, the entire surface of Mount Li was covered by a layer of greenish-gray turtle shell!
Mount Li was the size of a large state, so one could imagine how big this turtle shell was!
"You can attack now." Mo's voice came from within Mount Li.
Mo had not participated in the battle of Mount Li. It had only arrived after the battle ended.
After it entered Mount Li, it had already activated its strongest defense, adding a solid line of defense to Mount Li.
Nuwa wanted to test the strength of her strength, so she tried to attack Mo's turtle shell.
"Be careful!" Nuwa's eyes were filled with excitement.
"Whoosh …"
The huge Nuwa shadow began to change shape.
Crimson energy beams condensed into one, like a huge rose floating in the air.
"Hiss …"
All of the energy beams converged towards the root of the "rose", and as Nuwa pointed her finger downwards, the enormous rose smashed downwards.
"So terrifying!"
"So strong …"
"Goddess Nuwa wouldn't destroy Mount Li, would she?!"
When the thousands of living beings in Li Mountain saw this scene, their eyes were filled with worry.
"Don't be scared! The azure barrier above our heads is formed from the energy of Truth. No matter how powerful Mother Nuwa is, she probably isn't as strong as the Primordial Chaos Primordial Gods … "
"Wu —"
The rose formed by Wa's shadow had already pierced into the tortoiseshell.
The energy beams continued to converge at a single point, drilling madly into the tortoiseshell.
Sharp whistling sounds reverberated in the sky. Some of the birds flying high in the sky seemed to be drunk when they heard the sharp whistling sounds and flew in all directions.
The people of Li Mountain were protected by the tortoiseshell, so they were not hurt by the shrill sound. However, the sound that penetrated the tortoiseshell still made many people uncomfortable.
"Nuwa, your strength cannot penetrate my armor." An indifferent voice came from within Mount Li.
Nuwa's long hair fluttered even more violently.
Beams of energy shot up into the sky and converged downward at high speed …
She pushed her bloodline power to the limit.
The originally crimson Wah's Shadow had now turned a deep purple.
A destructive aura was constantly being released from within Wa's Shadow.
According to the information on the Blood Control Technique, Wa's Shadow possessed a powerful destructive power. If Wa's Shadow was pushed to the limit, it would trigger the Black Phoenix Candle …
"Let me see!
I want to feel the power of the Black Phoenix Candle!
Show it! "
Nuwa roared loudly.
Every muscle in her body was stretched taut. Her slender body was filled with power.
After the last trace of purple faded from Wa's Shadow, it suddenly split open and transformed into a deep black flame!
An endless destructive aura emanated from within.
"This is the Black Phoenix Candle!"
"Whoosh!"
A black phoenix descended from behind Nuwa and drilled into the tortoiseshell again.
The originally unmoving tortoiseshell's surface was now filled with cracks.
"Ka ka ka ka …"
The barrier of "Desert" was a whole.
If one part was not broken, the whole thing would not be broken.
If one part was broken, the whole thing would also shatter.
The cracks extended in all directions at an unbelievable speed. Then, it shattered with a loud bang …
The moment the green tortoiseshell shattered, another layer of dragon scales covered the surface of Li Mountain. This was the second layer of the barrier!
"Desert" had set up three layers of defenses on Li Mountain. After the outermost Mi 'an Tortoiseshell was broken, the second layer, "Gu Dragon Armor", was next.
"Not bad, you can break my Mi 'an Tortoiseshell. Stop, you can't break my Gu Dragon Armor," Mo said.
This was just a spar. Mo was not willing to use too much truth power.
However, Nuwa did not make a sound. She continued to urge the Black Phoenix Candle to drill down!
"Wu wu wu …"
The Gu Dragon Armor was much stronger than the Mi 'an Tortoiseshell.
However, under the corrosion of the Black Phoenix Candle, it actually showed signs of shattering.
After Mo felt the Black Phoenix Candle's increasing power, she actually panicked. "Enough, enough. Nuwa, this is just a spar …"
At this moment, Nuwa did not seem to hear anything.
Her eyes turned pitch black as if she could not control them!
"Ka …"
The Gu Dragon Armor shattered.
The third layer of the barrier quickly appeared on Li Mountain.
It was an ancient gray tortoiseshell. This was Mo's own tortoiseshell.
"Stop! Someone stop Nuwa! If this continues, both sides will suffer! "
It was not that Mo did not have any methods to counter Nuwa. Her tortoiseshell could reflect most of the energy.
However, if the Black Phoenix Candle's energy was reflected, it might kill Nuwa directly. Mo naturally did not want to see this happen.
At this moment, a figure silently appeared beside Nuwa. It was Fuxi.
"Hua!"
Nuwa did not even look. She waved her hand and the surging black flames rushed towards Fuxi.
Fuxi's expression did not change much. He opened and closed his hands. With a light circle, an invisible Eight Trigrams cycle was formed in his hands.
"Hualala …"
The destructive flames were like obedient children. They followed the Eight Trigrams cycle in Fuxi's hands and kept spinning.
The flames clearly did not want to be trapped in the small Eight Trigrams. However, no matter how it tried, it could not break out of the cycle.
When Fuxi placed his hand on Nuwa's shoulder, Nuwa's body trembled slightly. Only then did she wake up. However, her body was already exhausted. She went limp in Fuxi's arms.
Fuxi brought her back to the Jade Platform.
Donghuang Tai Yi, Rat King Sheng Tian, and Elder Ying looked at Nuwa with lingering fear.
Although the Blood Control Mystic Art could greatly improve the Immortal Realm, Nuwa's improvement was too great. It was so great that she could directly fight against a Chaos Ancient God.

Chapter 3716
The Immortal Realms on the Jade Platform had just seen the Memory Realm Crystal. The shocking scene of over ten thousand Evil Deities killing "Kong" was still fresh in their minds.
In just a short while, the scene in the Memory Realm Crystal had already been replayed. The wariness in their hearts was magnified to the extreme. They felt as though they were facing a great enemy!
Donghuang Tai Yi had his hands behind his back. A golden sword pointed to the sky from his back. Vast sword intent slowly emanated from his body. It was like wind and mist, endless and endless.
Elder Ying held her sword horizontally. The wrinkles on her face deepened. She stood still on the spot like a withered tree that had died many years ago. However, in that damaged body, there was a terrifying power contained within.
After grasping the blood control mystic technique, any Immortal Realm's strength was enough to dominate the past Mother World …
The number of Evil Deities was still increasing. They were densely packed and wanted to occupy the entire sky.
At this moment, the one who felt the most heartache was Nuwa!
Although these people were Evil Deities, they were also her Li Mountain disciples.
For every additional Evil Deities, there would be one less Li Mountain disciple …
How could Nuwa bear to kill her own disciple?
"Lord Ancient Gods, is there no way to stop the Evil Deities?"
Nuwa transmitted to everyone in a low voice.
Chang shook his head and replied, "His possession divine ability directly uses the Mother World. The living beings in the Mother World have innate karma. We … can't stop it."
"It's not impossible. However, I can only influence a few dozen people at most," Ji replied.
Ji could use the truth of life to influence the possession of Evil Deities. However, in front of the rapidly increasing number of Evil Deities, it was just a drop in the bucket.
"Hiss, hiss, hiss, hiss …"
Two streams of energy, one black and one white, flowed around Fuxi's feet. They formed an illusory eight trigram seal.
"Don't be soft-hearted." Fuxi's expression became unprecedentedly serious. "They are already enemies. This is a fact that no one can change!"
"Hu …"
The eight trigram seal formed an invisible power that revolved around Fuxi.
In the past, Fuxi's pursuit of power was very naked. He squeezed the power of his bloodline to the extreme in pursuit of the fastest and strongest destructive power.
After he cultivated the blood control technique, he had a new understanding of his own bloodline!
"Idiot, this isn't a matter of whether we're enemies or not. It's just that we have no chance of winning!"
Nuwa quietly glared at Fuxi.
"That's right. Killing them is useless. No matter how many we kill, the Evil Deities can be replenished in Mount Li. The ones we kill will always be our own people!"
Ji said.
This was an unequal battle.
If they wanted to fight against an invincible existence, it would be meaningless no matter how much energy they expended.
Luo Zheng stared at them for a while and then said, "Seniors, since there is no chance of winning, why don't we try our best to escape from the Mother World?"
The influence of Evil Deities was limited to the Mother World, so it shouldn't be able to radiate outside of the Mother World.
"Flee …" Ji analyzed. "Void is the best Primal Chaos Ancient God at escaping, but he still can't escape. It won't be that easy for us to escape from our mother world …"
After Ji saw the Memory Realm Crystal, he suggested that everyone leave their home world. This was indeed the best suggestion.
However, Evil Deities were monitoring Luo Zheng's movements at all times. They wouldn't give them a chance.
"Since this is the only way, we have to give it a try. Why don't the three Primal Chaos Ancient Gods give it a try? We can separate Mount Li from the Mother World!"
Luo Zheng transmitted his voice to everyone.
Ji, Chang, Mo, Fuxi, Nuwa, and the others' expressions changed slightly. This was probably the only way to avoid total annihilation …
"It's a way, but Evil Deities won't be so stupid as to just stand by and watch," Fuxi transmitted his voice.
"Lady Nuwa, use the Ring of Descent to inform Lord of Heaven Primordial Beginning. If necessary, he can descend to help. I'll go and stabilize Evil Deities first!"
Before returning to Mount Li, Luo Zheng gave two Rings of Descent to Lord of Heaven Primordial Beginning in case he needed them. Now, he had to use them so quickly.
After speaking, Luo Zheng left the Jade Platform with a whoosh and floated in midair.
Evil Deities were still possessing one body after another …
The number of people floating in midair had already exceeded ten thousand. However, this time, he seemed to want to summon even more Evil Deities.
He wasn't in a hurry at all.
As long as he was in the Mother World, he was invincible.
Whether it was Primal Chaos Ancient Gods, Immortal Realm experts, or Luo Zheng, they would all be trampled under his feet.
Some things were only interesting when they were done slowly!
"These Immortal Realm experts have all mastered the Secret Blood Controlling Technique. Luo Zheng, you should have it too, right?"
Ten thousand Evil Deities stared at Luo Zheng in unison. Their gazes seemed to weigh down on Luo Zheng, making him feel even more pressured.
However, the one who spoke was the leader of the Evil Deities.
"Yes," Luo Zheng replied.
Evil Deities' eyes lit up slightly. "Let me see your power!"
"You can try," Luo Zheng replied.
Evil Deities didn't hesitate in the slightest. He gently opened his palm, and a near-perfect fusion of Dao-Fusing energy was formed.
Luo Zheng stared at the complete fusion of Dao-Fusing energy, a cautious look appeared in his eyes.
Since he was currently stalling Evil Deities, he could put on a more convincing act.
"Is this the complete fusion of Dao-Fusing?"
Luo Zheng pretended to be "curious" and asked.
"Yes, this is the complete fusion of Dao-Fusing energy! I have yet to comprehend the Power of Truth from it. If you are willing to follow me, I can teach you without holding back, "Evil Deities said.
"Not interested!"
"You will be soon!"
Evil Deities' arm swayed slightly, and the fusion of Dao-Fusing energy elongated into a long sword. Then, his figure flickered, and he slashed at Luo Zheng's head.
Evil Deities was at the peak of the Holy Soul Realm, and combined with the might of the fusion of Dao-Fusing energy, he was far more threatening than many Immortal Realm experts.
The instant Evil Deities approached, Luo Zheng gently opened his palm, and a thread of blood shot out from his palm. The blood thread quickly wove together as if it had a mind of its own, and turned into a hollow blood sword.
Whoosh!
Whoosh!
A sword formed from Dao-Fusing energy and a sword formed from bloodlines slashed together.
Buzz — —
In just an instant, Luo Zheng and the Evil God had missed each other by a body's length.
In the next moment, Evil Deities' body turned into countless fragments and dissipated with the wind. Even the sword formed from Dao-Fusing energy was extinguished …
The other ten thousand or so Evil Deities were all shocked.
Evil Deities knew that he was no match for Luo Zheng alone, but he didn't expect the difference in strength to be so great.
The blood sword in Luo Zheng's hand was too special. The fusion of Dao-Fusing energy didn't even have time to explode before it was destroyed. What kind of bloodline power was this?
Evil Deities hadn't been to the Immortal Painting, so he didn't know much about bloodline powers. However, he instinctively sensed the uniqueness of Luo Zheng's bloodline!
If he devoured Luo Zheng, that bloodline power would definitely belong to him!
Evil Deities became even more excited when he thought of this!
"As expected of my master! Again! "
This time, two evil gods stepped out of the ranks, pincer attacking Luo Zheng from the left and right.
While Luo Zheng and Evil Deities were fighting, Desert's barrier was rapidly advancing underground.
That layer of Gu Long Armor was like a sharp circular blade, constantly separating Li Mountain from the Mother World.

Chapter 3717
With the power of a Primal Chaos Ancient God, it wasn't hard to cut open a large continent.
Previously, Desert had three layers of enchantments covering the top of Mount Li. Now, it was only guiding the enchantments underground.
After half an incense stick's worth of time, a huge circular ravine appeared outside the Mother World!
If the Mother World was like a watermelon, Li Mountain had already been cut open by the Gu Dragon Armor barrier.
As long as there was a slight push, Li Mountain would be able to leave the Mother World.
Above Li Mountain, dozens of Evil Deities were still fighting with Luo Zheng.
The Evil Deities enjoyed this kind of battle.
He had almost limitless power. This power was like a foot of white silk that slowly tightened around Luo Zheng's neck.
Only by constantly increasing the power could he force Luo Zheng to his limits and force Luo Zheng to submit to him in despair!
Sou sou sou …
Dozens of Evil Deities dragged their Dao Fusion energy and charged at Luo Zheng like a pack of wolves.
Luo Zheng appeared abnormally calm.
He knew that Mo was trying his best to cut open Li Mountain.
The longer this dragged on, the more advantageous it would be for Li Mountain.
Whoosh —
The blood sword in his hand lightly twisted and slashed in all directions.
The blood sword instantly underwent a complicated transformation. Each blood thread pierced through an Evil Deity's body and quickly formed a complicated and disorderly diagram.
As Luo Zheng fiercely pulled, these sharp diagrams quickly slashed through and dozens of Evil Deities were turned to pieces.
Blood rained down from the sky.
Nuwa's heart was trembling.
As the Master of Li Mountain, Nuwa had a natural leadership temperament and never lacked courage and decisiveness.
There would be sacrifices in any battle, but being sacrificed at the hands of one's own people was the most painful.
The Evil Deities possessed nearby bodies. The Evil Deities floating in the air were all elites of Li Mountain.
They didn't know what had happened and died at Luo Zheng's hands for no reason. Nuwa's regret couldn't be expressed with words.
"Li Mountain has been cut open!"
At this moment, Mo's voice entered everyone's ears from within Li Mountain.
Nuwa, Fuxi, Heaven Winning Rat King, Donghuang, and the two Primal Chaos Ancient Gods all frowned.
The first step was complete, and the second step was to push Li Shan out!
Luo Zheng's expression didn't change at all after hearing the voice. He was still focused on facing the impact of Evil Deities.
"You can even block that?! How about a hundred people? "
Evil Deities said excitedly.
Then, hundreds of Evil Deities rushed towards Luo Zheng.
To be precise, he was ranked 101st!
One of the Evil Deities directly teleported in front of Luo Zheng.
"Pu!"
Luo Zheng instinctively stabbed the Blood Sword into the chest of the Evil Deities and said, "This kind of attack is futile …"
Before the Evil Deities turned into pieces, he smiled and said, "I want to tell you that trying to move Li Shan out of the Mother World is also futile."
Luo Zheng's expression sank. He gently pulled the Blood Sword in his hand and dismembered the Evil Deities into pieces. He then shouted, "Seniors! Attack! "
"Boom! Boom! Boom!"
Three loud sounds could be heard from within Li Mountain.
At the same time, Nuwa, Fuxi, and the other Immortal Realm experts teleported away from the Jade Platform and appeared at the peak of Li Mountain, the waist of the mountain, and other places.
There was a circular barrier at each of these locations. These were the pressure points that Mo had set up beforehand.
Li Mountain's body was still very weak to Immortal Realm experts. These pressure points allowed them to use their strength evenly on the huge body of the mountain.
After the Immortal Realm experts appeared on the circular barrier, they used all their strength and attacked these pressure points.
Actually, given enough time, even a Primal Chaos Ancient God could 'push' Li Shan out of the Mother World.
However, they still had to face the variable of Evil Deities. They could only attack when they were unprepared!
The edges of Li Mountain had already been cut by Gu Long Jia's barrier. When the Immortal Realm experts and Primal Chaos Ancient Gods' strength exploded, there wasn't even a crack at the foot of Li Mountain. Instead, a circular, smooth fracture appeared!
"Hua …"
Countless amounts of Primal Chaos Aura swept in from the fracture at the foot of the mountain. The three barriers that Mo had set up had already enveloped the entire Li Mountain. These barriers could completely block the Primal Chaos Aura outside.
Li Mountain began to slowly sink!
"What happened!"
"Li Mountain is collapsing!"
"Such a huge mountain can't possibly collapse …"
Li Mountain had many human and Nuwa cities. These cities were built on the flat waist of the mountain. Suddenly, they felt that the entire mountain waist was sinking. This feeling was beyond their understanding.
More than ten thousand Evil Deities in the sky looked at the slowly sinking Li Mountain. The mockery in their eyes intensified.
"You have no chance of winning against an individual. Fighting against the Mother World is even more wishful thinking!"
After the Evil Deities finished speaking, the spatial crystal walls around Li Mountain instantly changed color.
The entire space within Li Mountain was sealed!
This kind of spatial lock was something that not even 'Sky' could break free from.
Li Mountain, which had sunk hundreds of feet, was now stuck …
"Truth bestow upon me greater strength!"
Silence roared loudly.
Narrow and Mo also began to crazily squeeze out their Truth Bloodline.
Fuxi stood at one of the focal points. His hair fluttered in the wind. The Eight Trigrams beneath his feet spun at an indiscernible speed.
Elder Ying, Rat King Sheng Tian, and Nuwa were the same …
"Ka!"
"Ka!"
"Ka!"
A loud ripping sound erupted around Li Mountain. It was as though two invisible gears had been dislocated.
And Li Mountain was sinking bit by bit amidst the ripping sound …
"The strength of your unity is very impressive. But look at who you are fighting against." More than ten thousand Evil Deities smiled elegantly at the same time.
Everything about Li Mountain was within his expectations. In fact, to add some fun, he didn't seal the space at first. Otherwise, Nuwa, Fuxi, and the other Immortal Realm experts would not have been able to scatter their forces around Li Mountain so easily.
"Weng weng weng weng weng …"
Azure spatial chains appeared on the surface of each Evil Deities. These spatial chains were like hidden weapons. They directly penetrated Mo's barrier and entered Li Mountain!
These spatial chains directly affected the entire mountain. There was no need for them to exert force!
Following which, the Evil Deities pulled the spatial chains and soared into the sky.
The Evil Deities originally had a simpler method to make Li Mountain stay, which was to kill the Immortal Realm experts and the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods with lightning speed. However, the tug-of-war with the entire Li Mountain was more interesting!
More than ten thousand Evil Deities were equivalent to more than ten thousand Saint Soul experts. The power they could unleash at the same time was no small matter!
In addition to the spatial seal of the Origin World, Li Mountain, which was sinking slowly, actually stopped!
Not only did it stop, but it was also slowly rising!
"Ka, ka, ka …"
The sound of the giant gears colliding resounded through the sky. Every time it rang, Li Mountain would rise by ten feet!
Not long later, the entire Li Mountain rose from the ground!
Such a huge mountain floating in the sky was something that had never happened before in the Origin World!
"If you like to fly, then fly higher …" The Evil Deities laughed.
"Weng, weng, weng …"
More Evil Deities appeared on the mountain peak. When these Evil Deities appeared, they had spatial chains in their hands.
More than ten thousand …
More than twenty thousand …
More than thirty thousand …
As the number of Evil Deities increased, Li Mountain also rose higher and higher!

Chapter 3722
These human heroes all looked at Du Zhen with concern.
"He's not dead." Du Zhen nodded. "The old man is alive and well."
Hearing this, the heroes were pleasantly surprised. The Primeval Lord of Heaven even breathed a deep sigh of relief.
When he heard the answer he wanted, a sense of relief welled up in his heart.
The human race in the 77th chaos era had not really declined at all!
The Primeval Lord of Heaven had led the heroes of the human race to survive until now, and Senior Brother was also safe and sound!
Their Junior Brother, Tongtian, was also fine!
Although Tongtian was on the opposite side, the three brothers were a miracle.
"Where has Senior Brother been all this time? Why did he disappear after entering Jade Clear Heaven?"
The Primeval Lord of Heaven asked again.
"It's a long story. After following Master into Jade Clear Heaven, we encountered unprecedented trouble …" Du Zhen briefly recounted what had happened in Jade Clear Heaven. That was a very long time ago.
"In order to prevent the Snake Spirit King from finding us, we've been hiding in the 'bubble' and rarely had a chance to leave. I'm taking a huge risk this time …"
"Then why are you here this time?"
Nuwa asked.
Du Zhen's gaze swept across the heroes as if he was looking for someone, but he did not find anyone among the human heroes. He said, "I'm here for Luo Zheng."
The Primeval Lord of Heaven, Nuwa, and the others all had a slight change.
With their wisdom, even if others did not say it, they could roughly guess it.
From the moment Luo Zheng entered the Heaven-splitting Divine Temple, he had shown extraordinary abilities.
When the Primeval Lord of Heaven and Nuwa's divided souls entered Luo Zheng's inner world, they found that Luo Zheng's inner world was a place of origin. Then, more bizarre things happened to Luo Zheng until Luo Zheng obtained the Origin Divine Blood.
This was too abnormal to be attributed to luck.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven and Nuwa's divided souls came to the conclusion that there was an extremely powerful driving force behind Luo Zheng.
But when the divided souls merged with the main body, the Primeval Lord of Heaven and Nuwa were even more confused!
What happened to Luo Zheng was something that even the Monkey Spirit King could not do.
What kind of powerful backer could help Luo Zheng fuse with the Origin Divine Blood?
This kind of thing was beyond the logic of imagination!
He couldn't figure it out even if he racked his brains!
"Don't tell me that Luo Zheng …" The Honoured Lord of the Origin stared at Du Zhen with a strange look.
Although the Honoured Lord of the Origin respected his Senior Brother very much, if Taishang Laojun was really the "driving force" behind Luo Zheng, it would be a big blow to the Honoured Lord of the Origin. This showed that Taishang Laojun was not on the same level as him.
"Yes, the Taishang Crafting Method that Luo Zheng practices is named after the word 'old man'," Du Zhen replied.
"The Supreme Artifact Refining Technique … The word 'Supreme' actually came from this." The Heavenly Lord of Primordial Beginning smiled bitterly and muttered, "Senior Brother's current abilities are far beyond my imagination …"
Nuwa pursed her lips. She had thought about the origin of the word 'Supreme' and had asked Luo Zheng about it. Even Luo Zheng himself didn't know, so she couldn't think of the missing Taishang Laojun.
"Uncle Master, you misunderstood me. I'm not as powerful as you think," Du Zhen said with a smile, "The Supreme Artifact Refining Technique was bestowed by a human spirit. It's not a supernatural power that I created …"
"Bestowed by a human spirit!"
The Honoured Lord of the Origin's white eyebrows trembled slightly. "Then the Origin Divine Blood …"
"It's also because of the Supreme Artifact Refining Technique," Du Zhen nodded.
"Then do you have the means to control the bloodline?"
The Honoured Lord of the Origin asked again. Although Luo Zheng had the blood control method, his control of the Origin Divine Blood was too shallow.
"That's why I'm here," Du Zhen nodded. "Can you bring Luo Zheng to the Nirvana Realm?"
The Honoured Lord of the Origin didn't say anything and took out the Ring of Descent.
…
Li Mountain drifted in the Chaotic World for a few days and didn't advance too far.
To the hundreds of millions of living beings in Li Mountain, it was already a complete change in appearance …
Although the drifting Chaotic Aura was blocked by the barrier, they still felt desolate and panicked after losing the support of their Mother World — even though most of them wouldn't be able to leave Li Mountain for the rest of their lives.
Nuwa ordered all the big cities on the west side of Li Mountain to move to the east side. In just a few days, the people in those big cities completed the migration.
Now, on the west side of Li Mountain, wisps of purple energy surrounded the mountain rocks and kept flowing. When the rocks came into contact with the purple energy, they either turned into gold or silver steel. At the same time, they kept changing their shapes.
That was the Power of Truth of the Chaos Ancient God, Narrow.
Li Mountain left in a hurry and didn't have any power. It was completely driven by the power of the three Chaos Ancient Gods. Its speed couldn't increase.
Narrow's Power of Truth could create matter at will and also change the shape of matter at will. It wanted to build Li Mountain into a huge ship to ensure that Li Mountain could travel long distances in the Chaotic World.
Luo Zheng stood on the west side of Li Mountain and watched Narrow transform bit by bit. He thought of the Divine Realm.
After being in the Mother World for so many years, he wondered where the Divine Realm was now.
After Narrow's transformation was completed, Luo Zheng would make a request to Nuwa and drive Li Mountain to search for the Divine Realm.
While he was lost in thought, the space beside him fluctuated and Fuxi appeared in front of Luo Zheng. "The Primeval Lord of Heaven wants to see you in the Nirvana Realm."
Luo Zheng nodded and sat down to recite the Sword Essence of Eternity. The next moment, he appeared in the Nirvana Realm.
"Senior Primeval Lord of Heaven." Luo Zheng stepped forward and cupped his hands.
"You were once confused about where the Grand Supreme Equipment Refining Technique came from. Now you know the answer." The Primeval Lord of Heaven smiled.
As soon as he said this, Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched. His gaze fell on the green-robed stranger not far away.
Gu Bei had found the Grand Supreme Equipment Refining Technique in the Chaos, but even Gu Bei couldn't say that it was a coincidence. After all, the probability of finding a palm-sized golden page in the vast Chaos was so small that it could be ignored.
"Your Excellency is …" Luo Zheng asked.
"My Master is the Grand Supreme Elderly Lord." Du Zhen cupped his hands and said, "I'm here on my Master's orders to help you get started on the path of cultivation."
"Get started on the path of cultivation?"
Luo Zheng asked, puzzled.
He had worked so hard to cultivate for so many years and finally reached the Immortal Realm. This guy thought that he hadn't gotten started yet?
The Primeval Lord of Heaven and the others were also confused.
"Yes." Du Zhen said with a serious face, "Can I enter your Inner World to take a look?"
Luo Zheng glanced at the Primeval Lord of Heaven. The Primeval Lord of Heaven nodded at him.
Then, Luo Zheng created an Inner Incarnation and let the Primeval Lord of Heaven, Nuwa, and Du Zhen's divided souls in.
Because the divided souls of the human heroes had been cultivating in Luo Zheng's Inner World, the Inner World as a whole was developing very well.
After looking around, Du Zhen sighed and said, "The old man was right. The Land of Origin is really desolate …"

Chapter 3729
Luo Zheng's words completely calmed them down.
Rashly taking revenge on the Snake Spirit Sect was no different from suicide.
"But what can we do?"
One of the human spirits asked.
This human spirit was called Tian Chu, he was the leader of this group of world builders, including Tian Chuan, the other spirits all obeyed him.
"Your understanding of the chaotic world is deeper than ours, to find the Human Spirit King, we cannot do without your help" Luo Zheng answered.
"Why should we trust you humans?"
Tian Chu stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Humans were created by the creators of these worlds.
In Tian Chu's eyes, Luo Zheng, Yuanshi Tianzun and the other humans were their sculptures, so how could a subworld's human spirit lead the main world's human spirit instead?
Actually, including Tian Chuan, the other human spirits all had the same thought.
It was normal for human spirits to have a sense of superiority in front of humans.
Luo Zheng didn't mind Tian Chu's doubt and smiled, "Because the Spirit Monkey King trusts me."
Tian Chu didn't say anything, the other human spirits also had nothing to say.
The world builders didn't have much status in the Four Spirit Sect, they couldn't even enter Shi Fang Prefecture.
The Spirit Monkey King chose to trust Luo Zheng, which was the biggest endorsement. These world builders were temporarily placed on Ye Ji Island.
…
In these few days, "Tang" worked tirelessly to modify Li Mountain.
A third of the entire west side of Li Mountain had already been dug out by "Tang", the excavated mountain rocks were all turned into metal by "Tang" using the power of truth. The metal was arranged according to a certain pattern, forming a huge circular structure that was several thousand miles long.
"Mo" set up a formation on the circular structure.
"The formation has been set up, it can be activated at any time" Mo told Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng took out the mother butterfly that Nuwa's main body gave him, and started to sense the position of the child butterflies.
One of the child butterflies was attached to his land of origin, according to Nuwa's main body, he could sense its position no matter how far away it was.
After he sent his consciousness into the mother butterfly, he felt a vague feeling in his mind, he felt a vague existence.
"North by northwest!"
"That direction!"
Luo Zheng pointed into the distance.
"Adjust the direction," Mo said to Chang.
"Rumble …"
The huge circular structure leaned against the side of Li Mountain, and Kong had created a huge turntable. Under Kong's control, the turntable led the huge circular structure to adjust its direction.
"That's all right," said Kuang.
At this moment, Mo and Solitary simultaneously released their Truth Force.
It wasn't just the two Primal Chaos Ancient Gods. Many Sempiternal Realm and Saint Soul Realm experts also released their bloodline power.
Massive amounts of energy converged towards the west of Mount Li. The energy was converging towards a tall tower. At the top of the tower sat a young girl. She was none other than Luo Yan.
She had already brought out her energy shaft.
When these violent and gentle energies converged on her, she would transform the energy axis.
After being assimilated by the energy shaft, the energy surged madly into the formation that Desert had set up on the enormous circular structure.
Buzz!
A huge amount of energy surged into the array, and the entire array began to flicker with intense light.
A huge pushing force was applied to the huge circular structure. The structure then evenly distributed the force to the mountain, pushing Li Shan to accelerate …
"Whoosh, whoosh …"
Li Shan began to adjust its direction.
This massive object left behind a long trail in the spacious chaos world. After adjusting its direction, it began to move at an extremely high speed towards the northwest of the chaos world.
Seeing Li Shan take off, Luo Zheng sighed, "It's a pity that we didn't meet the Sky Rending Hou. If we could directly pass through the void created by the Sky Rending Hou, we could probably save a lot of time …"
When Luo Zheng was heading towards her mother world from the God Domain, she had passed through the void created by the Sky Rending Hou, which greatly reduced the distance to her mother world.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Mo let out a faint laugh, "A mere void created by the Sky Rending Hou cannot allow Li Shan to pass through. If Space is still alive, a void created by him might be able to pass through."
The Sky Rending Hou was only one of the ten great chaos beasts. Its strength was only equivalent to an Immortal Realm expert. Primal Chaos Ancient Gods naturally didn't think much of it.
"Don't be anxious. With the array and Luo Yan's energy axis, Li Shan can reach an extremely high speed. Let's wait and see …" Mo said.
Luo Zheng nodded, "Having the help of three Ancient God seniors is humanity's greatest fortune."
Li Shan continued to accelerate.
The mother world was already very far away from Li Shan. It looked like a full moon hanging in the sky.
To the hundreds of millions of humans and Nuwa Clan members on Li Shan, these past few days had been abnormally fearful. They had never thought that Li Shan would completely leave their mother world and fly them to an unknown region. They also didn't know what fate awaited them in the future.
…
The world in Luo Zheng's body was still evolving at an extremely fast speed.
Every time a monstrous tsunami rose, a large piece of land would emerge from the sea.
At first, the land only appeared in bits and pieces, occupying only a small portion of the ocean. Not long after, more and more volcanoes erupted from the bottom of the ocean. The terrifying destructive power created monstrous tsunamis.
Piece after piece of land appeared like bamboo shoots after the rain, connecting together to form a large expanse.
All kinds of plants began to appear. A moment ago, it was still a barren land, but the next moment, it had become a lush grassland. The flow of time was so fast that it was suffocating.
According to the development trend of the previous world in his body, after the plants appeared, all kinds of living creatures would be born. The living creatures would slowly gain wisdom and establish tribes, cities, great forces, empires, and even civilizations …
Luo Zheng couldn't interfere with the development of the Land of Origin. He could only be a silent spectator.
But the situation didn't develop in the direction that Luo Zheng expected.
A kind of bamboo shoot plant was born on these lands. After these bamboo shoot plants were born, they began to madly devour other plants and began to grow infinitely.
The other plants on the lands seemed to be food for these bamboo shoot plants.
Not long after, all the plants on the lands were devoured by these bamboo shoot plants. These bamboo shoot plants rapidly grew from a few dozen feet to dozens of miles, then hundreds of miles, thousands of miles, all the way to the edge of the Land of Origin!
In the mother world, Luo Zheng had also seen some large plants. Some plants with wisdom could even cultivate to the Nirvana Realm. But none of them could be compared to these bamboo shoot plants.
When these bamboo shoot plants that were thousands of miles long and hundreds of miles in diameter gathered at the edge of the Land of Origin.
Luo Zheng frowned and looked for a while. His pupils shrank slightly, and two words appeared in his mind.
"Teeth?"

Chapter 3732
Luo Zheng did not restrain the Origin Divine Blood again, allowing it to turn into a blood mist and evaporate, dissipating in the sky above the Land of Origin.
Then the Land of Origin began to shake again, and a blood waterfall flowed out from the blood mist in the dome.
After these blood waterfalls fell from the dome all the way to the bottom, they condensed and formed blood pillars!
There were a total of three hundred and seventy-three blood pillars, and each blood pillar corresponded to a bloodline power of the Origin Divine Blood.
"Buzz …"
These blood pillars suddenly glowed with a bright blood light, and these blood lights immediately turned into long arrows and shot toward Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not dodge and let these blood light arrows hit him.
"Puff!"
After the first blood light arrow hit Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng felt something extra in his mind, which was the bloodline power.
An ability in the Creation Quadrant …
Before Luo Zheng had time to try this ability, more blood light arrows shot toward Luo Zheng.
"Puff! Puff! Puff! Puff … "
Every time a blood light arrow hit Luo Zheng, a bloodline power would appear in Luo Zheng's mind.
After more than ten breaths, all the blood light arrows entered Luo Zheng's body, and Luo Zheng's mind had the complete power of the Origin Divine Blood.
"The power of space …"
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and pointed.
Somewhere in the Land of Beginnings, layers of overlapping square spaces appeared.
These spaces were astonishingly stable, and were not inferior to the spaces built by "Sky" with the power of truth, or even better.
"The power of destruction! Explode! "
Flowers of flame exploded in those square spaces, and the power of destruction spread out. All the square spaces exploded at the same time, and the entire Land of Origin could feel the blazing heat.
"Creation …"
Luo Zheng spread out his hands across the air.
When the energy of the Creation Quadrant spread out, long swords appeared out of thin air around him.
Each of the long swords was not ordinary, but they were created out of thin air. Hundreds, thousands, tens of thousands of long swords fell like raindrops, colliding with each other and making crisp and pleasant sounds, quickly piling up into a sword mountain.
The Chaos Ancient God could also create things out of thin air, and Jiang Ziya could also do it with the Trueness of Three Spirits Construction, but the quality and speed of the things he created were far inferior to Luo Zheng's.
"Life …"
Luo Zheng's eyes stared down.
The emerald green life force began to gather, and hundreds of light spots the size of a person.
Soon, people walked out of the light spots one by one …
These people looked exactly like Luo Zheng, and their physical strength and strength were comparable to the Immortal Realm.
Although Luo Zheng could create many avatars on the continental plates, the avatars couldn't leave the inner world. These Luo Zhengs, on the other hand, were genuine 'humans' and could be released to participate in battle.
"Pick up the swords!"
Luo Zheng ordered, and all the "Luo Zheng" picked up the long swords on the ground, flicked their wrists, turned around, and slashed toward the sky.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Hundreds of extremely sharp silver sword lights rushed into the sky, slashing all the way to the dome of the Land of Origin before stopping.
Luo Zheng's face showed satisfaction when he achieved such power by using the most basic ability of the Origin Divine Blood.
…
In the Jade Clear Sky.
A group of gray beetles was flying close to the ground.
These gray beetles were very agile, almost perfectly avoiding all obstacles, always maintaining an inch of distance from the ground, and moving forward at high speed. If one did not observe carefully, one would not be able to detect them with the naked eye.
But in the magical land of the Jade Clear Sky, there was never a lack of magical Nirvana Realm creatures.
A kind of palm-sized traveler bird had unlimited vision, and no matter how hidden or small things were, they could not escape their eyes.
"Coo coo coo …"
When the traveler birds found the group of gray beetles flying close to the ground, they flapped their wings and swooped down.
The gray beetles' flying speed was not slow, but the traveler birds were obviously faster!
In the blink of an eye, the traveler birds had rushed into the group of gray beetles, constantly pecking the gray beetles into their mouths.
"These smelly birds …"
The gray beetles were Du Zhen's transformation. If he had not encountered these troublesome traveler birds, he could have used this form to turn back smoothly.
"Buzz!"
Among them, dozens of gray beetles' bodies were suffused with a golden color, and immediately swooped down on the traveler birds.
If he was in a normal state, Du Zhen would naturally not be afraid of these traveler birds.
But Master Taishang Laojun had told Du Zhen that he could not return to his original form in the Jade Clear Sky, not even for a breath, otherwise he would be caught by the Spirit Snake King.
The methods he could use in his beetle form were very limited, and now he could only make these gray beetles self-destruct!
"Bang bang bang bang bang …"
Those golden beetles that were pecked into the traveler birds' mouths quickly exploded, directly blowing the heads of the traveler birds to pieces!
"They are really looking for death …"
Du Zhen had just finished speaking when he suddenly heard more "coo coo" sounds. He looked up and was immediately dumbfounded.
The explosion just now had attracted more attention, and thousands of traveler birds rushed down.
The traveler birds that covered the sky swooped down, and in the end, there was only one gray beetle left in the group. If this gray beetle was pecked, Du Zhen's life would directly end here …
Fortunately, Du Zhen made a new change at the last moment. He quickly turned into a pangolin and plunged into the ground, narrowly avoiding disaster!
Sneaking in the form of a pangolin was naturally much safer, but the speed was greatly reduced. It took Du Zhen a few days to find the "air bubble".
He had just entered the air bubble and turned back into his original form, and called out with a tired face, "Old man …"
Taishang Laojun appeared beside Du Zhen and reached out to gently point at the entrance of the air bubble. The entrance quickly disappeared without a trace.
Seeing Taishang Laojun's swift movements, Du Zhen's face changed slightly. "Old man, someone is following me?"
Taishang Laojun nodded with a serious face. "There is a trace of an aura that does not belong to you. I'm afraid you are being watched …"
"How is that possible! I have never transformed into a human form, "Du Zhen said. The Nirvana Realm creature that he had transformed into could not be identified even with Divine Sense, so Du Zhen was very confident in his bloodline ability.
Moreover, the Snake Spirit Sect and the Bird Spirit Sect were in the midst of a fierce battle, so it was even more impossible for them to find them.
Taishang Laojun did not speak. He controlled the air bubble to move deeper into the ground.
In the past years, the "air bubble" had been discovered once or twice, but the "air bubble" itself was not a fixed cave and could move freely. After being discovered, it naturally hid in a more secluded place. On the other hand, Taishang Laojun was insignificant in the eyes of the Snake Spirit King. He was just a few rats hiding in the ground.
Now that the Snake Spirit King was preparing for a big purge, whether it was the high and mighty Bird Spirit King or Taishang Laojun hiding in the ground, they would all be eliminated together. Naturally, the treatment would be different from before.

Chapter 3733
When the bubble began to move, the other disciples of Taishang Laojun were alerted.
After all, this bubble had been underground for a long time, so it wouldn't act rashly under normal circumstances.
"Master, what's going on?"
"Master is moving the bubble. Has he been discovered?"
Taishang Laojun used to have many disciples. When he entered Jade Clear Sky, there were a total of four disciples.
Four of them died while exploring Jade Clear Sky. Another two were killed by the Snake Spirit Sect after leaving the bubble. Now, there were only four disciples left in the bubble. The eldest disciple was Du Zhen, who was good at transformation.
The second disciple, Du Zhen, had a huge body and a round belly. He always had a chubby face, like a Maitreya.
The third disciple, He Yun, was thin and had a layer of frost on his face. He looked serious.
The fourth disciple, Xuan Du, was tall and sturdy. He carried a purple-gold Eight Trigrams Furnace. There were flame tattoos on his right arm. The flame tattoos flickered and poured into the furnace.
The fire in the furnace hadn't been extinguished for thousands of years. He carried the furnace wherever he went.
Compared to Du Zhen, these three disciples were much more respectful to Taishang Laojun.
"Yes, we've been exposed!"
She Taishang Laojun answered briefly. He looked as if he was facing a great enemy and concentrated on moving the "bubble".
This "bubble" could isolate any aura and simulate the surrounding environment. Even if Divine Sense scanned it, it would be no different from mud. It was difficult to detect.
Once they had moved enough distance, they would be safe.
The bubble made a slight scraping sound as it moved underground.
Everyone had experienced this before, so they weren't too nervous. They just held their breath and stared at Taishang Laojun.
While Xuan Ke was moving, the sound of chewing came from the other side of the bubble.
。
"Kacha, kacha …" Hearing this sound, everyone's expression changed.
"It's a Gold Digging Beast!"
There were all kinds of strange creatures underground in Jade Clear Sky, and the Gold Digging Beast was one of them.
The Laughing Gold Digging Beast fed on metals. It dug out all kinds of metals underground and swallowed them all year round.
The most troublesome thing was that it was unusually sensitive to metals. Even the "bubble" couldn't block it. The "bubble" had been bitten by the Gold Digging Beast several times.
"Go chase it away!"
Du Zhen said.
"Senior Brother, you might not be able to defeat the Gold-Digging Beast in any other form …" Xuan Du shook his head.
"I agree." He Yun nodded.
During this special period, it was best that they didn't leave the bubble at all.
Then, they flew to the other side of the bubble with their True Forms. When they got close to the bubble, the gray edges of the bubble began to bulge. That was the Gold-Digging Beast trying to squeeze into the bubble.
Snake. "I'll catch it. You get ready," Xuan Du said as he approached the edge and pressed his hand against the bubble.
A Nirvana token slowly appeared behind Xuandu. It was an ink brush that was as tall as a person. The tip of the ink brush was not dipped in red ink, but in azure spatial power.
After Yan Xuandu's eyes were tightly shut, the ink brush began to draw in the air.
The blue spatial energy formed an outline in the air. It was the outline of the Gold Digging Beast.
When the outline of the Gold Digging Beast was fully formed, Xuan Du's eyes suddenly opened. He released a rich blue light.
However, the Gold Digging Beast obviously didn't expect this to happen. After it was pulled into the bubble, it was a little dumbfounded and stared blankly at the two humans in front of it.
The True Forms didn't give the Gold Digging Beast any time to react.
He opened his mouth and his mouth became huge in an instant.
The True Forms patted his belly and said with satisfaction, "Done!"
After the two of them dealt with the Gold Digging Beast, they returned to Taishang Laojun's side.
Taishang Laojun controlled the "bubble" to move a few thousand feet deeper. After moving a hundred thousand feet to the north, he finally stopped. At the same time, he let out a sigh of relief and asked Du Zhen, "How was the trip to Night Vision Island?"
"It went very smoothly. Just like what Master said, the Origin God Blood began to expand after entering the Land of Origin," Du Zhen said.
Taishang Laojun showed a hint of joy. "That's great!"

"Yes." The general of the Si Mansion stared at Shen Chengang and said, "Taking back the coordinates from the Snake Masters is harder than ascending to the heavens. The only chance is to gain their trust.
The three human palaces had always been sent to the Clear Sky Platform by the Snake Spirit Gate to guard some minor things.
The Si Mansion was cautious and conscientious. They had never expressed any dissatisfaction with the Snake Spirit Gate. They had always been loyal and devoted. However, this did not mean that the general of the Si Mansion could not see through the Snake Masters' ambitions.
"Don't you still want to become a Human Spirit King?" The general of the Black Palace asked. The general of the Si Mansion laughed and said, "These Snake Masters are all vicious. Once we have no use for our Human Spirits, why would they keep us around to cause trouble? The Monkey Spirits have all been cleaned up by them. When the Bird Spirit Gate is taken down, all of us Human Spirits will be cleaned up.
Even a three-year-old child would not believe such a childish promise to help me become a Human Spirit King! "
When these words were said, the disgust everyone had for the general of the Si Mansion suddenly disappeared.
"But your coordinates are still in the hands of the Sky Yin Snake Master," the general of the Black Palace said.
The general of the Si Mansion smiled faintly and said, "I do not intend to leave."
When the Sky Yin Snake Master took his coordinates, the general of the Si Mansion had already made up his mind.
"Phew!"
Shen Chengang looked at the general of the Si Mansion with a responsible expression and said, "All this time, we have wronged you."
"If you had not wronged me, how would you have gotten a chance?" the general of the Si Mansion said.
"But the other shore is only so big. Even if we escape with the coordinates, I'm afraid it will be difficult to find a place to stay," Shen Chengang said worriedly.
The general of the Si Mansion stared at Shen Chengang and asked, "Why do you think the Snake Spirit King suddenly wants to kill the Monkey Spirits?"
The Monkey Spirits in Sky Square City were not weak. They were much stronger than the three human palaces. Furthermore, the Monkey Spirits were very loyal and did not scheme. They were a powerful force in the hands of the Snake Spirit Gate.
The Snake Spirit King suddenly knocked out the coordinates of the Monkey Spirits in a hurry and completely cleaned them up. This was very strange in itself.
Shen Chengang could not understand the profoundness of this and shook his head.
"The Monkey Spirits are usually loyal to the Snake Spirit King. However, what if the Monkey Spirit King awakens?" the general of the Si Mansion asked.
"You mean …" Shen Chengang's eyes widened as he stared at the general of the Si Mansion in disbelief.
The general of the Si Mansion nodded and continued, "I saw a few humans on the Clear Sky Platform. They saved the world creator. I do not know if the Monkey Spirit King's awakening is related to those humans, but I suggest you go to the Night Vision Island!"
It was no secret that the Monkey Spirit King was imprisoned on the Night Vision Island. The human spirits also knew that the passage to the Night Vision Island had been destroyed. The general of the Si Mansion deduced the abnormality of the Night Vision Island based on these two factors. At this moment, the snake mark on the general of the Si Mansion's shoulder suddenly lit up.

Chapter 3738
The general covered the snake-shaped mark with his hand and said calmly, "Let's go!"
"But …" The general of the Black Manor hesitated.
"Let's go!"
The general of the Black Manor roared.
Shen Chengang looked deeply at the general of the Black Manor and ordered, "Human spirits of the Yue Manor, Guang Manor, and Black Manor, bring your coordinates and leave with me!"
"Whoosh!"
Shen Chengang took the lead and created a series of ripples on the water surface.
The other human spirits kept their coordinates and followed closely behind Shen Chengang.
The general of the Black Manor watched the human spirits leave and his mouth twitched. He released his hand and let the snake-shaped mark show.
"General of the Black Manor! What are you doing! Why aren't you attacking the nest! "
The Sky Yin Snake Lord roared angrily.
"Immediately," the general of the Black Manor replied.
"What happened? Why are you dragging this out?"
The Sky Yin Snake Lord asked.

"But you're the only one left?"
The Sky Yin Snake Lord realized that something was wrong through the snake-shaped mark.
"You talk too much …"
The general of the Black Manor reached out and touched the snake-shaped mark lightly. The snake-shaped mark broke into pieces.
The Sky Yin Snake Lord was suddenly reprimanded by the general of the Black Manor. It was stunned for a while before it roared angrily.
The Sky Yin Snake Lord could not confirm what had happened.
The thirteenth round of attacks had begun and it was too far away to do anything. It could only wait until it was over.
Taking advantage of the intense battle between the Snake Spirit Sect and the Bird Spirit Sect, the human spirits of the three manors quickly left the battlefield.
They went around the Fog City and the checkpoint of the Snake Spirit Sect, heading straight for the center of the Clear Jade Sky …
However, the Spirit Snake King saw all of this.
After the Snake Spirit Sect's thirteenth round of attacks, the Snake Spirit King finally appeared on the battlefield.
There was an amber-colored diamond-shaped gem embedded in Sin's head. This was a saint's item called the Limited Omniscient Stone.
This gem could see everything in a limited area. The Clear Jade Sky was a region, so nothing could escape its eyes.
When Du Zhen returned to the bubble, the Snake Spirit King had keenly noticed it. The bifid serpent had secretly sent a set of coordinates into the bubble. a
"There are some deserters," the Snake Spirit King said.
The Biheaded Serpent beside the Spirit Snake King revealed a look of surprise. "Who dares to escape?"
"The Three Palaces of Man and Spirit," the Snake Spirit King said.

The Snake Spirit King looked at the end of the wine pool and shook her head. "No need. The three human palaces are not important. The Bird Spirit King can no longer hold back. There is no need to pay attention to these trivial matters."
On the peninsula behind the wine pool, the Bird Spirits used rare golden silkworm silk to weave a huge bird's nest. On the nest, there was a huge bird. This bird looked like a swan, and every feather on its body was symmetrical and diamond-shaped. When it spread its wings, it looked like two magnificent crystal mines.
But this giant bird's head was prostrated on the ground, sticking out of the nest, motionless …
Its eyes were not closed. Instead, they were wide open, filled with chaos and killing intent.
"I feel threatened …"
"There are no innocents in this world. All sins should be cleansed by me …"
"Those snakes are the root of all sins …"
The giant bird was the Bird Spirit King, "Shou". For countless years, "Shou" had maintained this posture on the ground.
It had not lost its power. On the contrary, "Shou" was filled with the desire to destroy. Even after turning into a strange body, its fighting strength was still terrifying. This was why the Snake Spirit King had not dared to take it down.
"King! It's just some small noises. You don't have to do it yourself! "
"My Bird Spirit Sect's Bu Zhong will be able to eliminate them …"
"Yes! We can do it! "
The three Bird Lords prostrated in front of the Bird Spirit King and kept persuading it.
The leading Bird Lord was called Luan Gu, and he was ranked first among the Bird Lords. The second was Han Ge, and the third Bird Lord was called Ling Lou. He wore a layer of heavy armor, and did not look like a bird. Instead, he looked like a huge reptile.
These three Bird Lords were supposed to participate in the battle at the wine pool, but when the Bird Spirit King moved, they had to stay and persuade it.
The Bird Spirit King had not awakened. It was still in a muddle-headed state.
After it turned into a strange body, it was full of resentment towards everything in this world. It wanted to destroy everything it saw.
The Bird Lords present did not know how many times they were killed by the Bird Spirit King …
This endless slaughter lasted for many years. Perhaps the Bird Spirit King was tired of killing, or part of its obsession was satisfied. It slowly accepted its own kind and subordinates …
But the Bird Spirit King's obsession still destroyed everything.
Although the Bird Lords knew that "Shou" had gone crazy, as the people of "Shou", they were extremely loyal. They helped the Bird Spirit King fulfill its obsession at all costs. They really planned to destroy the entire chaotic world.
Only in this way could the Bird Lord wake up.
"But their endless charge … made my people suffer. I … want to clean them up …" Shou said in a dream-like voice.
"We can win it back. The strange body can be reborn infinitely. It's not difficult," Luan Gu said.
The Bird Spirit Sect was in big trouble. Luan Gu chose to cover up the truth.

Shou became very excited.
Lavender light flowed around each of Shou's wings.
"Hua Hua Hua …"
It raised its head and lifted its body.
The hard diamond-shaped feathers collided with each other. It was like the sound of a shell sprinkling on the beach, crisp and harsh.
Although its nest was still quite a distance away from the Wine Pool Battlefield, it could still see the Snake Spirit King reflected in its eyes.
Shou's obsession was deep.
It wanted to kill the Snake Spirit King the most, and then drag the whole world down with it.
One could imagine what kind of cruel despair it had experienced before it died.
"King! You can't act rashly! "
"The Snake Spirit King must be leading you out …"

The three Bird Lords were anxious.

"Hua Hua Hua …"
"Shou" flapped its wings.
Strands of strange purple light danced around its wings, and the surrounding space seemed to be in awe of it, constantly twisting to match its every move.
"Shua!"
Then the three Bird Lords saw the Bird Spirit King disappear in front of them, leaving only a purple streamer extending towards the Wine Pool Battlefield.

Chapter 3739
Among the four sect masters, the Spirit Bird King was the fastest at "Hunting" and had the most profound understanding of space.
The "deep space" was an obscure and undecipherable area for the creatures of the Main World.
Including the Four Spirit Sects, many creatures of the Main World had entered the deep space to explore, but only "Hunting" could explore that space with ease.
It had reached the unreachable extreme state of the deep space, so only "Hunting" could bring back the Origin Divine Blood.
The Spirit Snake King could observe "Sin's" every move through the limited omniscient stone. When the Spirit Snake King saw "Hunting" raise its head, it said, "It's going to attack …"
However, some speeds were so fast that it was incomprehensible. Even if you knew how the other party attacked, there was still no way to deal with it. This was the so-called "all magical powers can be broken, but only speed can't be broken."
After the Spirit Snake King issued a warning, the Biheaded Serpent also became extremely alert. The next moment, it and the Spirit Snake King saw streaks of purple flowing light.
One of the streaks of purple flowing light swept across the Biheaded Serpent's body. It only felt a slight chill, and then it saw a huge bubble-like bird claw appear in front of it.
This huge bird claw also flashed away. For nearly a breath, the Biheaded Serpent thought it was really an illusion.
But after a breath, a terrifying force pressed down heavily!
"Ka, ka, ka!"
With the Biheaded Serpent as the center, three ravines of thousands of feet long appeared. This was the footprint of the Spirit Bird King's claw. The Biheaded Serpent was trampled deep into the ground, and its body was directly trampled into several pieces by the claw.
However, although the power of the Spirit Bird King's claw was terrifying, it could not kill the Treacherous Body without the Light of Annihilation. The broken parts of the Biheaded Serpent squirmed like earthworms and slowly connected into a whole body.
The Biheaded Serpent could not avoid this claw, and neither could the Spirit Snake King.
After all, "Hunting's" main target was the Spirit Snake King. The purple flowing light directly wrapped around the Spirit Snake King, and then "Hunting's" huge body directly collided with the Spirit Snake King.
"Hua Hua Hua Hua Hua …"
The Spirit Snake King's Treacherous Body directly moved horizontally, rubbing against the wine pool battlefield as it retreated. The shallow water in the wine pool, which was knee-deep, was actually stirred up by the Spirit Snake King's huge body into a huge wave.
After the Spirit Snake King retreated a distance of thirty to forty thousand feet, it fiercely raised its tail and fiercely nailed it into the Wine Pool Battlefield. Only then did its rolling body stop …
The snake form had a natural weakness in battle.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
The purple light flowed from top to bottom, and the Spirit Bird King slowly descended from the sky, revealing its body.
A supreme majesty was released from the Spirit Bird King's body. Its dark and chaotic eyes were filled with indescribable hatred.
"Sin, you finally dared to come. I've been waiting for you …"
Shou's voice was very loud.
The high frequency vibrations caused the water surface of the entire Wine Pool Battlefield to tremble slightly.
The Spirit Snake King raised its huge body and silently stared at Shou. It could feel the undisguised killing intent from Shou.
Among the Four Spirit Sects, the Monkey Spirit Sect was reckless and warlike, the Human Spirit Sect was good at research, and the Bird Spirit Sect was the most transcendent and peaceful. The Spirit Bird King, Shou, regarded Ku Shu, Sin, and Yin as close friends.
Even if the internal struggle of the Four Spirit Sects was very fierce, Shou still tried his best to maintain the internal peace and stability.
It naively thought that as long as it gave everything, the Four Spirit Sects could be reconciled and united like the Four Spirit Sects in the beginning, facing one powerful foreign race after another.
In the Four Spirit Sects, Shou's contribution was the greatest. After all, even the foundation of the Four Spirit Sects was brought by it. However, Shou had never been arrogant, and instead contributed selflessly.
However, Shou's tolerance did not bring peace, but greater disputes.
What it did not expect was that the Spirit Snake King would destroy the entire Four Spirit Sects, and even the entire Main Realm …
After entering the Chaotic World, it understood that the culprit was the Spirit Snake King. Shou completely collapsed. After everything it insisted on was broken, Shou was controlled by despair.
This world was so ugly that the only way to purify it was to return everything to zero.
The Spirit Snake King was the one who needed to pay the price the most!
The purple flowing light flowed out of Shou's body. Wherever this light was, it belonged to it …
This was the case in the Chaotic World, in the Main Realm, and even in the deep space!
The spatial law it comprehended could be used from a higher level of space to a lower level of space. Its talent could be imagined.
"Hua …"
When the purple flowing light wrapped around the Spirit Snake King's body, Shou disappeared again.
"Ka, ka!"
With two muffled sounds, Shou's sharp claws had quietly grasped the Spirit Snake King.
"Whoosh!"
Then it grabbed the Spirit Snake King and flew to the other side of the wine pool.
When these two behemoths flew over the wine pool, no one on the battlefield could catch their figures. Even the Bifid Serpent, who watched the Spirit Bird King leave, did not know where they went.
The wine pool battlefield was located in the southernmost part of the Clear Jade Sky. With Shou's incredible speed, it almost reached the edge of the Clear Jade Sky in the blink of an eye.
"Swish!" The Spirit Snake King was thrown out of the Clear Jade Sky.
It had an obsession with destruction in its heart, and the simplest way to kill the Spirit Snake King was to throw it into the Void Dust and swallow it alive!
"Zizi …"
Even the Spirit Snake King's treacherous body could not resist the devouring of the Void Dust.
The Spirit Snake King's huge body rotated and twisted in the Void Dust like a leech sprinkled with salt. It struggled for a while before it was immediately swallowed up.
When the Spirit Snake King was swallowed, the killing intent in Shou's eyes weakened a little.
Although the Spirit Snake King was its biggest enemy, killing just one Spirit Snake King was not enough to awaken Shou.
It flapped its wings, and the purple streamer drifted to the other side. Just as it was about to leave with the purple streamer, a ball suddenly emerged not far away. The ball quickly expanded, revealing a golden coordinate.
The Spirit Snake King's body slowly drilled out of the golden coordinate. Even the limited omniscient stone on its forehead was intact. After it drilled out, the golden coordinate was wrapped in the ball and disappeared.
"You … won't die?"
There was some confusion in Shou's eyes.
"Hiss …"
"The guy who hasn't awakened is stupid," the Spirit Snake King sighed.
To kill Shift, one had to rely on the Light of Annihilation, and to completely kill Shift, one had to knock out its coordinates. This was not a secret in Clear Jade Sky, but Hunt acted as if he knew nothing about it.
"Then I'll kill you ten times, a hundred times …" Shou was once again surrounded by killing intent.

Chapter 3740
Shou's behavior was simple and direct.
Since "Sin" was the target it had to kill, it would repay it with endless slaughter.
"Hoo …"
Purple light filled half of the sky.
In this area, Shou's behavior was unpredictable.
From one place to another, Shou didn't fly over, nor did he open a space channel. He moved by jumping, suddenly disappearing and suddenly appearing, showing a completely incomprehensible space movement.
Incomprehensible meant that there was no way to counter it. As long as he was in the purple light, his speed was infinitely fast, and he couldn't escape!
Therefore, Shou disappeared after he finished speaking.
The Spirit Snake King only felt his body move, and the next moment, he was thrown into the void by Shou.
"Zi Zi Zi …"

Then the ball that wrapped the Spirit Snake King's coordinates appeared again.
