He knew the reason for the Spirit Snake King's resurrection should be related to the coordinates in the ball. With a gentle flap of his wings, the purple light rolled towards the ball.
As long as he was swept by the purple light, he could get the ball. To a certain extent, the purple light could be regarded as a part of Shou's body!
But at this moment, a strange scene appeared.
No matter how fast the purple light was, the ball could always avoid it. Its speed was exactly the same as the speed of the purple light. The two flew in a nearly parallel state.
After chasing for hundreds of miles, the Spirit Snake King's treacherous body had already emerged from the coordinates.
At this time, the ball disappeared, and the Spirit Snake King was once again enveloped by the purple light.
The same situation happened again. The Spirit Snake King didn't have time to react and was thrown into the void by Shou again.
But this time, under the struggle of the Snake Spirit King, the mockery in its crescent-shaped eyes was cast at the back of Shou, where a strange three-headed snake was crawling.
From the beginning, the Spirit Snake King knew that he couldn't compete with Shou in terms of speed, and there was even a gap in strength.
Although he had the terrifying power to defeat Shou, he didn't need to do so to deal with an unawakened madman. He only needed one Saint's item.
The three-headed snake was called the Source of Curses, and it was the second item of the Serpent Spirit King.
The Serpent Spirit King had used this artifact a long time ago. The Source of Pain could place three Pain Engravings on three targets. As long as the opponent killed one of the targets, a three-headed snake monster would silently appear on its back.
No one else could see the three-headed snake except for the Spirit Snake King herself.
Every time a target was killed, one of the serpent's heads would open its eyes. Once all three targets were killed, the three-headed serpent's curse would take effect.
In the past, the Spirit Snake King had to sacrifice three Spirit Snakes to use the three-headed snake monster.
Now that the Spirit Snake King had a Treacherous Body, she only needed to sacrifice herself three times …
"Hiss hiss hiss …"
The three-headed serpent opened its eyes, and the curse began to take effect.
Shou also noticed that something strange had appeared behind him. He flapped his wings, and his body was constantly jumping in the purple streamer. Under his crazy flight, his huge body was not coherent. He appeared at the far end for a while, and then appeared at the edge of the purple streamer.
As Shou's flying speed continued to increase, the Spirit Snake King saw countless figures of Shou flashing. These were not illusions, nor were they afterimages. In the state of high-speed jumping, each of them was the body of Shou. It was a very unique scene.
"It's useless. No matter how fast you are, you can't get rid of the Source of Curses …"
The Source of Curses was like a blooming evil flower.
The more it struggled, the more it bloomed. The three serpents frantically absorbed the power of Shou, growing stronger and then entangling Shou.
The area of the purple streamer quickly shrank. After a while, Shou fell from the sky.
"Poor thing …" The Spirit Snake King sighed. "Drag it into the Void Dust!"
Under normal circumstances, Shou could travel in the Void Dust without the help of the Spirit Boat. But now that the Source of Curses had absorbed its power, there was no other possibility but to be swallowed by the Void Dust.
"Hiss hiss hiss …"
Soon, Shou's Treacherous Body was dragged into the Void Dust by the three serpents and was quickly swallowed.
The battle between the Spirit Bird King and the Spirit Snake King came to an end.
"Limited Omniscient Stone."
The diamond-shaped gem on the Spirit Snake King's forehead flashed, and everything in Clear Jade Sky fell into its eyes.
In order to protect Shou, the Spirit Snake Sect would naturally hide Shou's coordinates.
But in Clear Jade Sky, there were too few things that could avoid the Limited Omniscient Stone. If there was one, it would be Taishang Laojun's Void Bubble.
After activating the Limited Omniscient Stone, the Spirit Snake King had already seen Shou's coordinates. Its serpent body suddenly bounced on the ground and flew toward the Wine Pool Battlefield.
In the middle and rear of the Wine Pool Battlefield, the three Bird Lords of the Spirit Bird Sect were fighting the Bifid Serpent.
The Luan Fox, Han Ge, and Ling Lou were originally chasing after the Spirit Bird King, but the Spirit Bird King was too fast. By the time they arrived, the Spirit Bird King and the Spirit Snake King had already disappeared. Only the Bifid Serpent remained in place.
The three Bird Lords were worried about Shou's safety. They originally didn't want to fight the Bifid Serpent, but they wanted to force the Bifid Serpent to tell them the whereabouts of the Spirit Bird King.
The Bifid Serpent didn't see where the Spirit Bird King took the Spirit Snake King. Even if it knew, it wouldn't say anything. The battle was naturally triggered.
Even the Luan Fox, a Bird Lord, was enough to fight the Bifid Serpent to a draw, not to mention the help of Han Ge and Ling Lou.
Under the siege of the three Bird Lords, the Bifid Serpent could only barely hold on.
At this moment, the Luan Fox's body suddenly trembled and froze in place …
"Luan Fox, what's wrong?"
Han Ge didn't understand.
The Luan Fox's right wing gently waved, and a coordinate flashed with golden light.
When Han Ge and Ling Lou saw the golden coordinate, their expressions suddenly changed.
"King …"
"He was killed!"
"He lost the battle with the Spirit Snake King?"
The three Bird Lords were dumbfounded.
This was the last thing they wanted to see.
Shou's strength was higher than the Spirit Snake King, but in the case of not awakening, plus the Spirit Snake King had a saint's item, Shou was likely to be at a disadvantage.
But they didn't expect the outcome to be decided so quickly. How long had it been and Shou was already killed …
The Bifid Serpent was even more overjoyed when it saw this scene. It, who was originally retreating, suddenly gained great courage. Its body suddenly bounced and flew like a shuttle towards the coordinates on the Luan Fox's wing.
That was the coordinates of Shou, the only life of Shou. If it was destroyed, the Spirit Bird King would completely fall. How could the Luan Fox let the Bifid Serpent touch it so easily?
The Luan Fox closed its wings and rushed into the sky!
But just as it was about to rush into the air, a huge shadow shrouded it.

Chapter 3741
Although the Luan Fox's reaction was fast, it was still not as fast as the Spirit Snake King.
Before it could dodge, it was swept by the Spirit Snake King's giant tail and fell heavily.
"Boom!"
A large crater appeared on the ground of the wine pool. The Luan Fox's body was torn apart, and the golden coordinates hidden under its wings were exposed.
Han Ge and Ling Lou looked at each other, spread their wings, and rushed toward the golden coordinates.
They were very loyal and would give up everything to protect their king.
"Bang, bang!"
Before the two bird masters could get close, they were directly sent flying by the Spirit Snake King.
The Spirit Snake King's long tail gently rolled up the golden coordinates in front of itself and put them into its mouth.
It was not easy for ordinary people to destroy the coordinates. They would need a special space hammer to do so.
However, it was not difficult for the Spirit Snake King. It put the golden coordinates in its mouth and muttered, "My most loyal and honest friend, farewell …"
Just as the Spirit Snake King was about to bite, a black light suddenly flashed from its tail.
The moment the black light was about to hit it, the Spirit Snake King's expression suddenly became abnormally frightened. Its originally relaxed snake body became as stiff as a zombie at this moment.
"Woo …"
The black light at the end of its tail continued to spread and swam along the Spirit Snake King's body.
The Body of Eerie had no bones, including the Spirit Snake King. However, the black light forcefully outlined a clearly visible spine from the inside, extending all the way to the Spirit Snake King's head, outlining the Spirit Snake King's skull.
"You can't kill it …" the Spirit Snake King said.
"Why?"
The Spirit Snake King asked angrily.
"Because Hunt is still useful," the Spirit Snake King replied.
"But it will ruin things …" the Spirit Snake King became more and more angry.
"I said you can't, so you can't …"
As the Spirit Snake King spoke, it suddenly spat out the golden coordinates in its mouth toward Han Ge.
The golden coordinates with great inertia knocked Han Ge to the ground.
"Me!"
The Spirit Snake King was very unwilling to give up and rushed toward the golden coordinates again, but before it could take two steps, its body stiffened again. It turned its body in the opposite direction and left the wine pool battlefield.
This scene stunned the Bifid Serpent, Han Ge, Ling Lou, and the Luan Fox. They did not know what had happened.
"King! King! "
The Bifid Serpent followed behind the Spirit Snake King.
"Retreat!"
The Snake Spirit King replied concisely.
"But now that we've reached this stage, we can completely annihilate the Bird Spirit Sect. But now …"
The Biheaded Serpent said unwillingly.
But halfway through its words, the Snake Spirit King suddenly turned its head, its pair of crescent eyes filled with a gloomy black light. "You want to disobey me?"
The two-headed serpent was the second-in-command of the Snake Spirit Sect. It had given everything it had to the Snake Spirit King and had always been loyal. This was probably the most serious warning the Snake Spirit King had ever given it.
"I, I … don't dare!"
Biju Orochi stammered for a while, then reluctantly gave the order to retreat.
In fact, since the battle at the Wine Pool, both sides had exhausted most of their Enchanters. Even if the Two-headed Serpent did not give the order, the Enchanters would naturally return after they died …
The fierce battle in Jade Clear Sky ended in such a strange way.
…
Ultimate Graveyard, Divine Clock City.
It was time to ring the bell once a month. Every month at this time, the group of Nirvana Realm cultivators would be unusually excited.
Many Nirvana Realm experts began to ring the bell according to the order.
During the process of ringing the bell this time, Hua Tianming was the one who received the most attention.
Ever since Hua Tianming stepped onto the 32nd step of the Heaven and Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda, the entire Ultimate Graveyard was shaken.
He suddenly became everyone's hope to reach the top!
After obtaining the Nightmare Stone, Hua Tianming's cultivation speed almost doubled. He had just stepped onto the 32nd step not long ago, so it was only logical for him to challenge the 33rd step in such a short time.
"Dong —"
After a long and melodious sound of the bell, Hua Tianming once again sat below the Divine Clock.
He closed his eyes and entered the Heaven and Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda.
Hua Tianming liked the atmosphere in the pagoda very much. Every time he entered, he felt a sense of joy. He had once thought that the owner of this pagoda should be a cheerful person.
However, when he entered this time, Hua Tianming acutely sensed that there was a subtle change in the atmosphere. A solemn atmosphere hovered in the pagoda.
'Is it because I reached the top?'
Hua Tianming thought silently.
He had already reached the 33rd step, and his gaze was fixed on the "lone sword".
The atmosphere in the Heaven and Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda was indeed very solemn.
Taishang Laojun and his four disciples had already gathered, silently watching Hua Tianming.
"Let Hua Tianming bear that sword," said Du Zhen.
"I agree," said Zhen Xing.
"I have no objections …" Xuan Du nodded.
"Master has the final say," said He Yun.
Taishang Laojun nodded. "Back then, something happened to the lone sword, which was why it became a useless sword. Now, Luo Nian has already modified the lone sword. According to Luo Nian, he connected the lone sword to the 11th step, and its power is incomparable to before. Do you regret it?"
The four disciples had been trapped in the "bubble" for a long time, and many of their ambitions seemed to have been extinguished. They nodded in unison.
"No," said Du Zhen. "Moreover, the reason we deliberately nurtured Hua Tianming and guided him to the 33rd step was because of this lone sword. Now that we've taken away Hua Tianming, what do you think?"
"But Luo Nian is still in the main world. Although there's no news of him now, what if he tries to send a message through the lone sword?"
Asked Zhen Xing.
Taishang Laojun had already prepared for this. He smiled faintly, and with a flip of his hand, a blood-red jade pendant appeared.
When the disciples saw this, their expressions changed slightly. Du Zhen even lost his voice. "Old man, this Bloodsoul Jade Pendant …"
This jade pendant was formed from the bloodline of Taishang Laojun, and had been nourished in his body for countless years. It was a clone created by Taishang Laojun, and the power it contained was comparable to the original body.
"Each of you turn into a soul fragment, and I'll merge it into the lone sword," said Taishang Laojun.
Du Zhen, Zhen Xing, and the others were puzzled. They didn't know why Taishang Laojun suddenly made such an arrangement. Xuan Du asked, "Master, why did you make such an arrangement?"
Normally, people would leave behind a soul fragment as a backup in the face of great danger. They should be safe hiding in the bubble.
Taishang Laojun's expression became heavy. "Last time, after Du Zhen returned and exposed our location, I felt uneasy."
"The bubble can't be tracked. We've already changed our location. Those troublesome gold-digging beasts have also been swallowed by Zhen Xing," said Xuan Du. He felt that there was nothing wrong with this plan.
Taishang Laojun sighed softly. "I know, but I've been feeling uneasy these past few days. Turning a soul fragment can always be a precaution."
Although the four disciples didn't understand Taishang Laojun's worries, they still followed their master's orders and each drew a soul fragment from their minds and entered the jade pendant.
Then, Taishang Laojun reached out and hung the jade pendant on the lone sword. The moment the jade pendant was hung, it melted into blood lines that attached to the sword. Soon, the blood lines slowly seeped into the lone sword.
Hua Tianming couldn't see Taishang Laojun and his disciples, nor could he hear them speak. He couldn't even see the process of the jade pendant merging into the lone sword.
After not seeing it for a while, the lone sword's aura changed again. Previously, it looked so broken, and the sword's aura didn't exist. It really looked like an ordinary weapon, but now, the energy contained in the lone sword was very shocking …
Hua Tianming couldn't figure out what had happened during this time, but his interest in the lone sword was still as strong as before.
"Hum, hum, hum …"
The big bell in the Divine Clock City began to ring intermittently. The time was almost up.
Hua Tianming smiled and reached out to grab the lone sword. When the sound ended, he would automatically leave the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda.
However, the moment Hua Tianming turned around, his expression suddenly changed. An immortal-looking old man appeared in front of him!
"You are …" Hua Tianming wanted to ask who the old man was, but after thinking about it, he said, "Are you the owner of the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda?"
Taishang Laojun nodded.
"You appeared because I reached the top?"
Hua Tianming asked.
Hua Tianming wasn't too surprised to see the owner of the Heaven Earth Mysterious Yellow Pagoda. He even felt that it was inevitable.
Taishang Laojun shook his head and said, "I want you to pass a message to Luo Xiao."
"Pass a message to Xiao Sheng …"
Hua Tianming felt very strange. He had climbed to the 33rd step with great difficulty and finally met the owner of the pagoda. Why was it that he wanted to pass a message to Luo Zheng's father?
"What message?"
Hua Tianming asked.
A small butterfly appeared in Taishang Laojun's hand. He casually threw it, and the butterfly flew down and stopped on the eighth step of the pagoda.
"Let Luo Xiao enter the pagoda by striking the bell and carrying this butterfly," Taishang Laojun said.

Chapter 3742
When he left the Black Yellow Pagoda, Hua Tianming was still in a daze.
'I've finally reached the top. Shouldn't the Pagoda Master congratulate me or assign me some tasks?
At the very least, he should uncover the truth of the Black Yellow Pagoda's existence or the meaning of the Ultimate Graveyard's existence? '
'Some other benefits would be fine too …'
However, the old man did not say anything and only asked Luo Xiao to pass a message. What was going on?
Hua Tianming opened his eyes under the big bell.

Although Hua Tianming's achievements in the Divine Clock City were remarkable and almost never disappointed anyone, Hua Tianming had reached the top this time after all. Everyone was looking forward to it but also a little worried.

Gu Bei, who was not far away, looked concerned.

After bearing the Paramita Token in his inner world and establishing a real link, that Paramita Token would belong to him.
Hua Tianming nodded and stood up, but his eyes were fixed on Luo Xiao in the crowd.
Although Luo Xiao was a Saint in the Divine Territory, he still had to queue up to ring the bell in the Divine Clock City.

Hua Tianming asked.

Hua Tianming was a living being born in the world within Luo Xiao's body. Later on, he was reborn and cultivated to a perfected True God in a short period of time. After entering the Sinkhole Graveyard, he headed straight for the thirty-third step.
On the other hand, Luo Xiao was still at the eighth level of the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda, and it was only equivalent to the eighth level of the Paramita Realm. As a saint of the God Domain, he'd fallen far behind now.
Of course, Luo Xiao was not the only one with this problem. Other than Gu Bei, the other Sages were not much better off. In fact, their situation was even worse.
Moreover, the reason why Hua Tianming was able to reach the 33rd step was because of the help from Lord Laojun and Du Zhen. However, even Hua Tianming didn't know about this, let alone outsiders. They only knew that Hua Tianming's talent was heaven-defying.
"After I reached the 33rd step, an old man appeared," Hua Tianming said seriously.
To the side, Gu Bei, Saint Jiu, and many other humans revealed strange expressions.
A strange light flashed in Luo Xiao's eyes. "Is he the master of the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda?"
Luo Xiao had learned more about the Paramita Realm from his wife, Li Luoshui. He knew that even in the Mother World, it was not easy to cultivate to the Paramita Realm. In the Nine Li Tribe, only Chi You had been to the Paramita Realm 33.
In everyone's eyes, the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda was equivalent to a separate Paramita Realm. How powerful was its master?
"He claimed to be." Hua Tianming nodded.
Luo Xiao, Gu Bei, Saint Jiu, and the others' expressions changed slightly again. Gu Bei even asked, "What did that old man say?"
Gu Bei was now considered a member of the upper echelons of the Sinkhole Graveyard, but he still knew very little about it. Not only Gu Bei, but even the natives of the Sinkhole Graveyard did not know much about this colossus.
"He asked Firmament Saint to go to the eighth level of the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda to carry a butterfly-like Paramita Realm token," Hua Tianming answered.

"That old man just put it in," Hua Tianming answered.
Luo Xiao's eyes lit up.
That was the Paramita Realm token that the master of the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda had put in. It must be extraordinary!
The Paramita Realm cultivators in the Divine Clock City who were still queuing up made way for Luo Xiao, allowing him to ring the bell in advance.
Ring the bell, enter the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda, find the butterfly …
After bringing the butterfly back to the Divine Clock City, Luo Xiao could not wait to carry it.
This was an extremely new Paramita Realm token.
However, when he tried to activate the butterfly's ability after carrying it, he realized that the butterfly was pitifully weak. This Paramita Realm token only had the strength of about ten Divine Jun and had no other special abilities!
"What's the use of this Paramita Realm token?"
Luo Xiao said gloomily.
This butterfly floated alone in the sky above Luo Xiao's Dayan Realm. It was very agile, but its ability was not up to its appearance.
Saint Jiu, Gu Bei, and Hua Tianming looked at each other. This butterfly was much weaker than the other Paramita Realm tokens at the eighth level of the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda.
"The strange thing is, why must Brother Luo Xiao carry the Paramita Realm token?" Saint Jiu said as he stroked his beard.
"Since it's the master of the Mysterious Yellow Pagoda's request, Brother Xiao should carry this Paramita Realm token for the time being. It's very likely that there are other intentions," Gu Bei said.
Luo Xiao nodded.
Even carrying this Paramita Realm token would not hinder his cultivation. It would not be too late to change it in the future when he reached a higher level.
…
After a period of acceleration, Li Shan's current speed was hundreds of times faster than before.
When this gigantic creature passed through the Chaos clouds, it stirred up the clouds and caused them to roll and tumble.

In the Void Bubble.

Taishang Laojun had done everything he could.

Chapter 3743
As the True Form laughed maniacally, it released a completely different aura. It was the aura of Treachery!

Treachery, who was hiding in the True Form's body, had already taken control of the True Form. However, the powerful Light of Destruction exploded in the True Form's body, making it feel very uncomfortable.
Even though the Resentment Level Treachery and the Hatred Level Treachery had a certain tolerance for the Light of Destruction, they were still very sensitive to it.

Under the unbearable situation, white balls of Treachery crawled out of the True Form's ears!

..... that's too..... ".

. Xuandu waved the huge ink brush. Blue spatial power flowed on the tip of the brush.

Mono-Colored thorns emerged from the Lunar Python's body and shot toward Taishang Laojun. Bai Ling, on the other hand, released icicles filled with Yin energy. These icicles covered almost all directions. For a moment, the interior of the originally small bubble was filled with cold air.
Taishang Laojun and his four disciples were all in the Immortal Realm, but they were much stronger than ordinary Immortal Realm cultivators.
Elder Xue had taught Taishang Laojun the Blood Controlling Secret Technique when he bestowed the dreamscape.

An even more exaggerated smile appeared on True Form's smiling face.

Senior Brother Du Zhen's body changed again. Apart from the spiky scales, his head began to shrink. His hands turned into the sharp claws of a wild beast. He crawled on the ground and shot out. He turned into a streak of black light and chased after the two snake masters!

However, the snake masters were also very fast. One black and two white ribbons of light moved crazily inside the bubble. They chased after each other for a few breaths of time, but they didn't collide!

Hum!
As two azure-blue lights flashed, Moon Python and Bai Ling, who were originally flying around in the bubble, were pulled in front of Xuandu.

Smack! Smack!
With two crisp sounds, the talismans stuck to Moon Python and Bai Ling's bodies. The Light of Annihilation was constantly released, causing the two snake masters to tremble uncontrollably.
They tried their best to shake off the talismans on their bodies. However, the talismans were specially made to deal with Bizarro Beasts. How could they be easily shaken off?
No matter how hard they struggled, the talismans stuck to their bodies like maggots. They couldn't shake them off no matter how hard they tried.
However, Moon Python and Bai Ling were Bizarro Beasts after all. Ordinary Light of Annihilation could only suppress them. It couldn't cause any substantial damage to them.
Xuandu took the opportunity to draw a few strokes on their bodies with his brush. The azure-blue spatial power transformed into ropes that tightly bound them.
At that moment, He Yun said, "True Form, quickly find the coordinates in your stomach!"
Only by knocking out the coordinates hidden in the True Form's stomach could the two snake masters be completely wiped out.

Chapter 3744
It was a risk for the snake masters to put the coordinates inside the Gold Digging Beast. After all, the coordinates were their lifeblood.

The Mysterious Yellow Pagoda released a powerful suction force and sucked the four disciples in.

The real end isn't in this world, "the bifid serpent said.
The Elemental Spirit civilization would report all important information to the Two-Headed Serpent, and the Two-Headed Serpent would choose the most important information to pass to the Serpent King.
Compared to the Spirit Snake King, the Two-headed Serpent had more information.
It could roughly guess that the reason why those humans were able to advance by leaps and bounds and even awaken the Spirit Monkey King was definitely due to the efforts of these few humans.
Old Lord Taishang laughed. Every wrinkle on his face was trembling. He said, "Do you think you understand the real end game?
Not to mention you, even Sin is muddle-headed! "
The biheaded serpent mocked. "You've been underground for too long, and you're so scared that you're spouting nonsense?"
In the eyes of the snake masters, what kind of wise king was Sin?
From the internal strife of the Four Spirits Sects, to the setup of the trap, and now, every step was extremely precise, so how could it be possible that they were muddle-headed?
..... . You're watching this, right? "
Taishang Laojun suddenly said.
A trace of coldness appeared in the eyes of the bifid serpent, and spikes emerged from its body. It did not want this old man of the human race to offend the Snake Spirit King with his nonsense. How could he say the name "Sin"?
Just as the bifid serpent was about to pounce on Taishang Laojun, a voice appeared in its mind.
"Let him speak."
"But my King, this old man …"
"Don't worry, I don't care."
Why would a truly powerful existence care about the words of a weak person?
The Spirit Snake Spirit King used the limited omniscient stone to spy on everything in the bubble. It wanted to hear what Taishang Laojun wanted to say.
The bifid serpent could only say to Taishang Laojun, "My King allows you to speak!"
Taishang Laojun sneered. "The Four Spirit Sects have been deceived. You've all been deceived …"

The bifid serpent helped the Spirit Snake Spirit King.
"The matter of demotion," Taishang Laojun said.
Hearing Taishang Laojun mention the word demotion, the eyes of the bifid serpent became more serious.
According to the bifid serpent's thoughts, the rats hiding underground had only been able to survive for so many years because they had accidentally obtained the bubble that protected them. What could these rats know?

The other snake masters didn't react. They didn't know what demotion meant.

Sin had told it the meaning of demotion. The Chaotic World and the other Subworlds were the result of the expansion of the Main Realm.
The reason why the Snake Spirit Sect was able to defeat the other three of the Four Spirit Sects was because they had made arrangements in the Chaotic World in advance. They took the initiative in the demoted world and won the battle.

Taishang Laojun said again.

Taishang Laojun used to set the Final Realm as his goal. However, after walking around in Elder Xue's dream, he realized that the Chaotic World was far from a simple Final Realm.
